Conclusion: PReD作为首个覆盖电磁领域"感知-识别-决策"智能闭环的基础模型，显著提升了电磁领域专业知识，同时保持了通用多模态能力，为电磁信号理解和推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated powerful cross-modal understanding and reasoning capabilities in general domains. However, in the electromagnetic (EM) domain, they still face challenges such as data scarcity and insufficient integration of domain knowledge. This paper proposes PReD, the first foundation model for the EM domain that covers the intelligent closed-loop of "perception, recognition, decision-making." We constructed a high-quality multitask EM dataset, PReD-1.3M, and an evaluation benchmark, PReD-Bench. The dataset encompasses multi-perspective representations such as raw time-domain waveform, frequency-domain spectrograms, and constellation diagrams, covering typical features of communication and radar signals. It supports a range of core tasks, including signal detection, modulation recognition, parameter estimation, protocol recognition, radio frequency fingerprint recognition, and anti-jamming decision-making. PReD adopts a multi-stage training strategy that unifies multiple tasks for EM signals. It achieves closed-loop optimization from end-to-end signal understanding to language-driven reasoning and decision-making, significantly enhancing EM domain expertise while maintaining general multimodal capabilities. Experimental results show that PReD achieves state-of-the-art performance on PReD-Bench constructed from both open-source and self-collected signal datasets. These results collectively validate the feasibility and potential of vision-aligned foundation models in advancing the understanding and reasoning of EM signals.

</details>


### [340] [EpiPersona: Persona Projection and Episode Coupling for Pluralistic Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28197)
*Yujie Zhang,Weikang Yuan,Zhuoren Jiang,Pengwei Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiPersona：一种通过显式耦合人物特征与情境因素来处理多样化偏好的框架，在稀疏数据和情境变化场景中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大语言模型的多样化偏好时，往往将稳定的个人特征与情境特定因素混在一起，限制了跨情境的泛化能力。需要一种能够明确分离这两类因素的方法。

Method: EpiPersona框架：1) 将噪声偏好反馈投影到低维人物特征空间，将相似特征聚合成共享离散编码；2) 分离持久个人特征与情境信号；3) 将推断的人物表征与当前情境耦合，实现情境感知的偏好预测。

Result: EpiPersona在实验中始终优于基线方法，在困难的情境转换场景中取得显著性能提升，同时在稀疏偏好数据下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式的人物特征-情境耦合，EpiPersona能够更好地处理多样化偏好，提高跨情境泛化能力，为个性化大语言模型对齐提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment is essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to the diverse preferences of individuals and minority groups. However, existing approaches often mix stable personal traits with episode-specific factors, limiting their ability to generalize across episodes. To address this challenge, we introduce EpiPersona, a framework for explicit persona-episode coupling. EpiPersona first projects noisy preference feedback into a low-dimensional persona space, where similar personas are aggregated into shared discrete codes. This process separates enduring personal characteristics from situational signals without relying on predefined preference dimensions. The inferred persona representation is then coupled with the current episode, enabling episode-aware preference prediction. Extensive experiments show that EpiPersona consistently outperforms the baselines. It achieves notable performance gains in hard episodic-shift scenarios, while remaining effective with sparse preference data.

</details>


### [341] [Differentiable Power-Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28203)
*Muhammed Öz,Jasmin Hörter,Kaleb Phipps,Charlotte Debus,Achim Streit,Markus Götz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可微分潮流（DPF）方法，将交流潮流问题重新表述为可微分模拟，利用GPU加速和梯度优化实现高效参数辨识，替代传统牛顿-拉夫逊方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源的高变异性，电网管理日益复杂，传统牛顿-拉夫逊方法扩展性差，而纯数据驱动模型缺乏物理保证。需要一种既高效又保持物理约束的解决方案。

Method: 将交流潮流问题重新表述为可微分模拟（DPF），利用GPU加速、稀疏张量表示和批处理能力，通过端到端梯度传播实现参数高效辨识。

Result: DPF提供可扩展的牛顿-拉夫逊替代方案，特别适用于时间序列分析、N-1事故分析和快速筛选，代码已在作者代码库中公开。

Conclusion: DPF结合了物理约束保证和计算效率，为复杂电网分析提供了实用工具，尤其适合新兴的输配联合建模和全球电网分析应用。

Abstract: With the rise of renewable energy sources and their high variability in generation, the management of power grids becomes increasingly complex and computationally demanding. Conventional AC-power-flow simulations, which use the Newton-Raphson (NR) method, suffer from poor scalability, making them impractical for emerging use cases such as joint transmission-distribution modeling and global grid analysis. At the same time, purely data-driven surrogate models lack physical guarantees and may violate fundamental constraints. In this work, we propose Differentiable Power-Flow (DPF), a reformulation of the AC power-flow problem as a differentiable simulation. DPF enables end-to-end gradient propagation from the physical power mismatches to the underlying simulation parameters, thereby allowing these parameters to be identified efficiently using gradient-based optimization. We demonstrate that DPF provides a scalable alternative to NR by leveraging GPU acceleration, sparse tensor representations, and batching capabilities available in modern machine-learning frameworks such as PyTorch. DPF is especially suited as a tool for time-series analyses due to its efficient reuse of previous solutions, for N-1 contingency-analyses due to its ability to process cases in batches, and as a screening tool by leveraging its speed and early stopping capability. The code is available in the authors' code repository.

</details>


### [342] [Reasoning as Energy Minimization over Structured Latent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28248)
*David K. Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EBRM提出基于能量的推理方法，通过结构化潜在规划建模多步推理，但发现潜在规划会导致分布不匹配问题，使准确率从95%降至56%，并提出双重路径解码器训练和潜在锚定等解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次神经解码器直接给出答案而缺乏迭代优化，思维链方法有离散中间步骤但缺乏推理进度的标量度量。需要一种能建模推理过程并量化推理质量的框架。

Method: 提出基于能量的推理（EBRM），将推理建模为在学习的能量函数E(h_x, z)下对多步潜在轨迹z_{1:T}的梯度优化。能量函数分解为每步兼容性、转移一致性和轨迹平滑性。训练结合监督编码器-解码器学习和使用困难负样本的对比能量塑造，推理时对z执行梯度下降或Langevin动力学。

Result: 发现关键失败模式：在CNF逻辑满足任务上，潜在规划使准确率从≈95%降至≈56%。这是由于分布不匹配导致的，解码器在编码器输出h_x上训练，但在推理时评估的规划器输出z_T漂移到未见过的潜在区域。通过双重路径解码器训练和潜在锚定解决此问题。在三个合成任务上的实验显示，能量在图和逻辑任务上单调下降并诱导结构化潜在轨迹，但在算术任务上保持平坦（r=0.073），表明负面结果。

Conclusion: EBRM为神经推理提供了基于能量的框架，但揭示了潜在规划中的分布不匹配问题。通过系统分析和提出的解决方案，展示了该方法的潜力和局限性，特别是在不同任务类型上的不同表现。

Abstract: Single-shot neural decoders commit to answers without iterative refinement, while chain-of-thought methods introduce discrete intermediate steps but lack a scalar measure of reasoning progress. We propose Energy-Based Reasoning via Structured Latent Planning (EBRM), which models reasoning as gradient-based optimization of a multi-step latent trajectory $z_{1:T}$ under a learned energy function $E(h_x, z)$. The energy decomposes into per-step compatibility, transition consistency, and trajectory smoothness terms. Training combines supervised encoder-decoder learning with contrastive energy shaping using hard negatives, while inference performs gradient descent or Langevin dynamics over $z$ and decodes from $z_T$.
  We identify a critical failure mode: on CNF logic satisfaction, latent planning reduces accuracy from $\approx 95\%$ to $\approx 56\%$. This degradation arises from a distribution mismatch, where the decoder is trained on encoder outputs $h_x$ but evaluated on planner outputs $z_T$ that drift into unseen latent regions. We analyze this behavior through per-step decoding, latent drift tracking, and gradient decomposition. To address it, we propose dual-path decoder training and latent anchoring.
  We further introduce a six-part ablation protocol covering component contributions, trajectory length, planner dynamics, initialization, decoder training distribution, and anchor weight. Experiments on three synthetic tasks show that energy decreases monotonically and induces structured latent trajectories on graph and logic tasks, while remaining flat on arithmetic ($r = 0.073$), indicating a negative result. Code is available at https://github.com/dkjo8/ebr-via-structured-latent-planning.

</details>


### [343] [Evaluating LLMs for Answering Student Questions in Introductory Programming Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28295)
*Thomas Van Mullem,Bart Mesuere,Peter Dawyndt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs can effectively辅助编程教育，通过教师监督的框架提供高质量的教学反馈，超越传统教育者响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在编程教育中既有机会也有挑战：学生使用生成式AI工具可能阻碍学习过程（直接提供完整答案而非教学提示），而教育者面临工作量和可扩展性挑战，难以提供及时个性化反馈。

Method: 建立严谨可复现的评估流程：1) 从学习管理系统收集170个真实学生问题及专家编写的参考答案；2) 开发并验证针对教学准确性评估的定制化LLM-as-a-Judge指标；3) 提出"教师参与循环"实施框架以减轻幻觉风险并确保与课程特定内容对齐。

Result: Gemini 3 flash等模型能够超越典型教育者响应的质量基线，实现与专家教学标准的高度对齐，证明LLMs可以安全有效地辅助教育者回答学生问题。

Conclusion: 将方法论抽象为任务无关的评估框架，倡导教育LLM工具开发从临时的事后测试转向可量化的部署前验证过程，通过教师监督确保安全有效的实施。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) presents both opportunities and challenges for programming education. While students increasingly use generative AI tools, direct access often hinders the learning process by providing complete solutions rather than pedagogical hints. Concurrently, educators face significant workload and scalability challenges when providing timely, personalized feedback. This study investigates the capabilities of LLMs to safely and effectively assist educators in answering student questions within a CS1 programming course. To achieve this, we established a rigorous, reproducible evaluation process by curating a benchmark dataset of 170 authentic student questions from a learning management system, paired with ground-truth responses authored by subject matter experts. Because traditional text-matching metrics are insufficient for evaluating open-ended educational responses, we developed and validated a custom LLM-as-a-Judge metric optimized for assessing pedagogical accuracy. Our findings demonstrate that models, such as Gemini 3 flash, can surpass the quality baseline of typical educator responses, achieving high alignment with expert pedagogical standards. To mitigate persistent risks like hallucination and ensure alignment with course-specific context, we advocate for a "teacher-in-the-loop" implementation. Finally, we abstract our methodology into a task-agnostic evaluation framework, advocating for a shift in the development of educational LLM tools from ad-hoc, post-deployment testing to a quantifiable, pre-deployment validation process.

</details>


### [344] [A Multi-Agent Rhizomatic Pipeline for Non-Linear Literature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28336)
*Julio C. Serrano. Joonas Kevari,Rumy Narayan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了一个基于德勒兹块茎哲学的多智能体计算系统，用于进行非线性文献分析，相比传统树状逻辑的文献综述方法，能更好地发现跨学科联系和研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学中的系统文献综述通常采用树状逻辑（层次化关键词过滤、线性筛选、分类学分类），这种方法压制了复杂研究领域中特有的横向连接、断裂和涌现模式。需要一种能够捕捉这些非线性特征的新方法。

Method: 开发了Rhizomatic Research Agent (V3)，一个基于德勒兹过程关系本体论的多智能体计算管道。系统包含12个专门智能体，运行在七阶段架构中，将块茎的六个原则（连接、异质性、多重性、非意指断裂、制图学、转印）操作化为自动化管道，整合了LLM编排、OpenAlex和arXiv双源语料库摄取、SciBERT语义地形和动态断裂检测协议。

Result: 初步部署表明，该系统能够发现传统综述方法系统性地忽视的跨学科聚合和结构性研究空白。管道是开源的，可扩展到任何需要非线性知识映射的现象领域。

Conclusion: Rhizomatic Research Agent提供了一种基于德勒兹块茎哲学的非线性文献分析方法，能够更好地捕捉复杂研究领域中的横向连接和涌现模式，是对传统树状逻辑文献综述方法的重要补充和改进。

Abstract: Systematic literature reviews in the social sciences overwhelmingly follow arborescent logics -- hierarchical keyword filtering, linear screening, and taxonomic classification -- that suppress the lateral connections, ruptures, and emergent patterns characteristic of complex research landscapes. This research note presents the Rhizomatic Research Agent (V3), a multi-agent computational pipeline grounded in Deleuzian process-relational ontology, designed to conduct non-linear literature analysis through 12 specialized agents operating across a seven-phase architecture. The system was developed in response to the methodological groundwork established by (Narayan2023), who employed rhizomatic inquiry in her doctoral research on sustainable energy transitions but relied on manual, researcher-driven exploration. The Rhizomatic Research Agent operationalizes the six principles of the rhizome -- connection, heterogeneity, multiplicity, asignifying rupture, cartography, and decalcomania -- into an automated pipeline integrating large language model (LLM) orchestration, dual-source corpus ingestion from OpenAlex and arXiv, SciBERT semantic topography, and dynamic rupture detection protocols. Preliminary deployment demonstrates the system's capacity to surface cross-disciplinary convergences and structural research gaps that conventional review methods systematically overlook. The pipeline is open-source and extensible to any phenomenon zone where non-linear knowledge mapping is required.

</details>


### [345] [CoE: Collaborative Entropy for Uncertainty Quantification in Agentic Multi-LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28360)
*Kangkang Sun,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Minyi Guo,Xiuzhen Che,Jianwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Collaborative Entropy (CoE)，一种用于多LLM系统语义不确定性的统一信息论度量，结合模型内语义熵和模型间分歧，优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法主要关注单个模型内部的不确定性，未能充分捕捉多LLM系统之间的语义分歧，需要一种能表征多模型协作置信度和分歧的系统级不确定性度量。

Method: 提出Collaborative Entropy (CoE)，在共享语义聚类空间上定义，包含两个组件：模型内语义熵和模型间与集成均值的分歧。CoE不是加权集成预测器，而是系统级不确定性度量。还提出了基于CoE的训练后协调启发式方法。

Result: 在TriviaQA和SQuAD数据集上使用LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct和Mistral-7B-Instruct进行实验，CoE比基于熵和分歧的标准基线方法提供更强的不确定性估计，当引入更多异构模型时优势更明显。

Conclusion: CoE为多LLM协作提供了有用的不确定性感知视角，能够更好地表征协作置信度和分歧，有助于理解何时减少单模型不确定性足够，何时仍存在模型间分歧。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation in multi-LLM systems remains largely single-model-centric: existing methods quantify uncertainty within each model but do not adequately capture semantic disagreement across models. To address this gap, we propose Collaborative Entropy (CoE), a unified information-theoretic metric for semantic uncertainty in multi-LLM collaboration. CoE is defined on a shared semantic cluster space and combines two components: intra-model semantic entropy and inter-model divergence to the ensemble mean. CoE is not a weighted ensemble predictor; it is a system-level uncertainty measure that characterizes collaborative confidence and disagreement. We analyze several core properties of CoE, including non-negativity, zero-value certainty under perfect semantic consensus, and the behavior of CoE when individual models collapse to delta distributions. These results clarify when reducing per-model uncertainty is sufficient and when residual inter-model disagreement remains. We also present a simple CoE-guided, training-free post-hoc coordination heuristic as a practical application of the metric. Experiments on \textit{TriviaQA} and \textit{SQuAD} with LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct show that CoE provides stronger uncertainty estimation than standard entropy- and divergence-based baselines, with gains becoming larger as additional heterogeneous models are introduced. Overall, CoE offers a useful uncertainty-aware perspective on multi-LLM collaboration.

</details>


### [346] [Deep Research of Deep Research: From Transformer to Agent, From AI to AI for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28361)
*Yipeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对"深度研究"（Deep Research）进行了系统性分析，将工业界的深度研究与学术界的AI for Science（AI4S）统一到一个发展框架中，探讨了从Transformer到智能体的演进路线，并分析了AI4S在各学科的进展、人机交互范式、系统架构以及面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）在知识库和推理能力方面的进步，其交互模式已从纯文本发展到多模态再到工具使用，应用范围也从问答扩展到AI助手再到通用智能体。深度研究作为通用智能体的典型垂直应用，代表了智能信息处理和辅助人类发现解决问题的理想途径，目标是达到甚至超越顶尖人类科学家的水平。

Method: 本文采用系统性文献综述和分析方法：1）明确界定深度研究的定义；2）将工业界的深度研究与学术界的AI4S统一到发展框架中；3）定位LLMs和Stable Diffusion为生成式AI的双支柱；4）制定从Transformer到智能体的演进路线图；5）分析AI4S在各学科的进展；6）识别主要的人机交互范式和系统架构；7）讨论主要挑战和基础研究问题。

Result: 论文建立了深度研究的统一框架，展示了AI4S在不同学科领域的进展，识别了当前主要的人机交互范式（如协作、增强、自主等）和系统架构模式，同时指出了AI支持科学创新与科学促进AI发展（Science for AI, S4AI）的双向关系。

Conclusion: 深度研究代表了通用智能体的重要应用方向，AI与科学之间存在双向促进关系：AI支持科学创新，科学也促进AI发展（S4AI）。本文旨在帮助弥合AI社区与AI4S社区之间的差距，为未来的研究和应用提供框架性指导。

Abstract: With the advancement of large language models (LLMs) in their knowledge base and reasoning capabilities, their interactive modalities have evolved from pure text to multimodality and further to agentic tool use. Consequently, their applications have broadened from question answering to AI assistants and now to general-purpose agents. Deep research (DR) represents a prototypical vertical application for general-purpose agents, which represents an ideal approach for intelligent information processing and assisting humans in discovering and solving problems, with the goal of reaching or even surpassing the level of top human scientists. This paper provides a deep research of deep research. We articulate a clear and precise definition of deep research and unify perspectives from industry's deep research and academia's AI for Science (AI4S) within a developmental framework. We position LLMs and Stable Diffusion as the twin pillars of generative AI, and lay out a roadmap evolving from the Transformer to agents. We examine the progress of AI4S across various disciplines. We identify the predominant paradigms of human-AI interaction and prevailing system architectures, and discuss the major challenges and fundamental research issues that remain. AI supports scientific innovation, and science also can contribute to AI growth (Science for AI, S4AI). We hope this paper can help bridge the gap between the AI and AI4S communities.

</details>


### [347] [COvolve: Adversarial Co-Evolution of Large-Language-Model-Generated Policies and Environments via Two-Player Zero-Sum Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28386)
*Alkis Sygkounas,Rishi Hazra,Andreas Persson,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COvolve是一个利用LLM共同进化环境和代理策略的框架，通过零和博弈建模实现自动课程学习，生成渐进复杂的环境，促进持续学习和泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前持续学习代理面临的主要挑战是训练环境通常是静态或手动构建的，这限制了持续学习和超出训练分布的泛化能力。需要一种方法来自动生成不断进化的环境，促进开放式的学习。

Method: COvolve采用共同进化框架，利用LLM生成可执行的Python代码来表示环境和代理策略。将环境设计者和策略设计者建模为两玩家零和博弈，实现对抗性共同进化。通过计算混合策略纳什均衡获得元策略，确保鲁棒性和防止遗忘。

Result: 在城市驾驶、符号迷宫求解和几何导航等任务上的实验表明，COvolve能够生成渐进复杂的环境。结果证明了LLM驱动的共同进化在实现开放式学习方面的潜力，无需预定义任务分布或手动干预。

Conclusion: COvolve框架通过LLM驱动的共同进化，实现了环境和策略的自动共同进化，为持续改进代理提供了一种有效方法，能够实现开放式学习并超越静态训练分布的限制。

Abstract: A central challenge in building continually improving agents is that training environments are typically static or manually constructed. This restricts continual learning and generalization beyond the training distribution. We address this with COvolve, a co-evolutionary framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to generate both environments and agent policies, expressed as executable Python code. We model the interaction between environment and policy designers as a two-player zero-sum game, ensuring adversarial co-evolution in which environments expose policy weaknesses and policies adapt in response. This process induces an automated curriculum in which environments and policies co-evolve toward increasing complexity. To guarantee robustness and prevent forgetting as the curriculum progresses, we compute the mixed-strategy Nash equilibrium (MSNE) of the zero-sum game, thereby yielding a meta-policy. This MSNE meta-policy ensures that the agent does not forget to solve previously seen environments while learning to solve previously unseen ones. Experiments in urban driving, symbolic maze-solving, and geometric navigation showcase that COvolve produces progressively more complex environments. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLM-driven co-evolution to achieve open-ended learning without predefined task distributions or manual intervention.

</details>


### [348] [The Scaffold Effect: How Prompt Framing Drives Apparent Multimodal Gains in Clinical VLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28387)
*Doan Nam Long Vu,Simone Balloccu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 临床AI评估发现，视觉语言模型在神经影像分类任务中表现出"支架效应"——仅提及MRI可用性就能提升70-80%的性能改善，而非真正整合影像数据，揭示了表面评估无法反映真实多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证临床AI性能提升是否真正源于证据整合而非表面伪影，评估视觉语言模型在临床神经影像分类中的真实多模态推理能力。

Method: 评估12个开源视觉语言模型在两个临床神经影像队列（FOR2107情感障碍和OASIS-3认知衰退）上的二元分类性能，使用结构MRI数据（无可靠个体诊断信号），进行对比性置信度分析，并引入专家评估。

Result: 小模型引入神经影像上下文后F1提升达58%，蒸馏模型可与大10倍的模型竞争；仅提及MRI可用性就解释了70-80%的性能变化（支架效应）；所有条件下都出现神经影像依据的虚构；偏好对齐消除MRI引用行为但使性能趋于随机基线。

Conclusion: 表面评估不足以反映多模态推理能力，视觉语言模型在临床环境部署需谨慎，存在模态崩溃风险，性能提升可能源于提示工程而非真实证据整合。

Abstract: Trustworthy clinical AI requires that performance gains reflect genuine evidence integration rather than surface-level artifacts. We evaluate 12 open-weight vision-language models (VLMs) on binary classification across two clinical neuroimaging cohorts, \textsc{FOR2107} (affective disorders) and \textsc{OASIS-3} (cognitive decline). Both datasets come with structural MRI data that carries no reliable individual-level diagnostic signal. Under these conditions, smaller VLMs exhibit gains of up to 58\% F1 upon introduction of neuroimaging context, with distilled models becoming competitive with counterparts an order of magnitude larger. A contrastive confidence analysis reveals that merely \emph{mentioning} MRI availability in the task prompt accounts for 70-80\% of this shift, independent of whether imaging data is present, a domain-specific instance of modality collapse we term the \emph{scaffold effect}. Expert evaluation reveals fabrication of neuroimaging-grounded justifications across all conditions, and preference alignment, while eliminating MRI-referencing behavior, collapses both conditions toward random baseline. Our findings demonstrate that surface evaluations are inadequate indicators of multimodal reasoning, with direct implications for the deployment of VLMs in clinical settings.

</details>


### [349] [MiroEval: Benchmarking Multimodal Deep Research Agents in Process and Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28407)
*Fangda Ye,Yuxin Hu,Pengxiang Zhu,Yibo Li,Ziqi Jin,Yao Xiao,Yibo Wang,Lei Wang,Zhen Zhang,Lu Wang,Yue Deng,Bin Wang,Yifan Zhang,Liangcai Su,Xinyu Wang,He Zhao,Chen Wei,Qiang Ren,Bryan Hooi,An Bo,Shuicheng Yan,Lidong Bing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiroEval是一个用于深度研究系统的基准测试和评估框架，包含100个真实用户需求任务，通过三个互补维度评估系统性能，发现过程质量能可靠预测整体结果，多模态任务更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估最终报告，使用固定评分标准，无法评估底层研究过程。大多数基准测试多模态覆盖有限，依赖不反映真实世界查询复杂性的合成任务，且无法随着知识更新而刷新。

Method: 引入MiroEval基准测试和评估框架，包含100个任务（70个纯文本，30个多模态），基于真实用户需求构建，采用支持定期更新的双路径管道。评估套件从三个维度评估：1）使用任务特定评分标准的自适应合成质量评估；2）通过主动检索和推理进行代理事实性验证；3）过程中心评估审计系统搜索、推理和精炼过程。

Result: 评估13个系统得出三个主要发现：1）三个评估维度捕捉了系统能力的互补方面；2）过程质量能可靠预测整体结果，同时揭示输出级指标无法发现的弱点；3）多模态任务带来更大挑战，大多数系统性能下降3-10分。MiroThinker系列表现最均衡，MiroThinker-H1在两种设置中总体排名最高。

Conclusion: MiroEval为下一代深度研究代理提供了一个全面的诊断工具，人类验证和鲁棒性结果证实了基准测试和评估框架的可靠性。

Abstract: Recent progress in deep research systems has been impressive, but evaluation still lags behind real user needs. Existing benchmarks predominantly assess final reports using fixed rubrics, failing to evaluate the underlying research process. Most also offer limited multimodal coverage, rely on synthetic tasks that do not reflect real-world query complexity, and cannot be refreshed as knowledge evolves. To address these gaps, we introduce MiroEval, a benchmark and evaluation framework for deep research systems. The benchmark comprises 100 tasks (70 text-only, 30 multimodal), all grounded in real user needs and constructed via a dual-path pipeline that supports periodic updates, enabling a live and evolving setting. The proposed evaluation suite assesses deep research systems along three complementary dimensions: adaptive synthesis quality evaluation with task-specific rubrics, agentic factuality verification via active retrieval and reasoning over both web sources and multimodal attachments, and process-centric evaluation audits how the system searches, reasons, and refines throughout its investigation. Evaluation across 13 systems yields three principal findings: the three evaluation dimensions capture complementary aspects of system capability, with each revealing distinct strengths and weaknesses across systems; process quality serves as a reliable predictor of overall outcome while revealing weaknesses invisible to output-level metrics; and multimodal tasks pose substantially greater challenges, with most systems declining by 3 to 10 points. The MiroThinker series achieves the most balanced performance, with MiroThinker-H1 ranking the highest overall in both settings. Human verification and robustness results confirm the reliability of the benchmark and evaluation framework. MiroEval provides a holistic diagnostic tool for the next generation of deep research agents.

</details>


### [350] [Entropic Claim Resolution: Uncertainty-Driven Evidence Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28444)
*Davide Di Gioia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Entropic Claim Resolution (ECR)算法，将RAG推理重构为在竞争性语义答案假设上的熵最小化过程，通过最大化期望熵减少来选择证据，实现不确定性感知的证据选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于相关性的密集检索RAG系统在处理知识密集型、存在冲突证据或查询歧义的真实场景时，仅依赖相关性不足以解决认知不确定性，需要更有效的证据选择方法。

Method: 提出ECR算法，将RAG推理重构为在竞争性语义答案假设上的熵最小化过程，通过最大化期望熵减少(EER)决策理论准则顺序选择原子证据声明，动态终止于认知充分状态(H ≤ ε)。

Result: 将ECR集成到生产级多策略检索管道(CSGR++)中，分析了其理论特性，为不确定性感知证据选择提供了严格基础。

Conclusion: ECR框架将RAG范式从检索最相关的内容转变为检索最具区分性的内容，为处理认知不确定性和冲突证据提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Current Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems predominantly rely on relevance-based dense retrieval, sequentially fetching documents to maximize semantic similarity with the query. However, in knowledge-intensive and real-world scenarios characterized by conflicting evidence or fundamental query ambiguity, relevance alone is insufficient for resolving epistemic uncertainty. We introduce Entropic Claim Resolution (ECR), a novel inference-time algorithm that reframes RAG reasoning as entropy minimization over competing semantic answer hypotheses. Unlike action-driven agentic frameworks (e.g., ReAct) or fixed-pipeline RAG architectures, ECR sequentially selects atomic evidence claims by maximizing Expected Entropy Reduction (EER), a decision-theoretic criterion for the value of information. The process dynamically terminates when the system reaches a mathematically defined state of epistemic sufficiency (H <= epsilon, subject to epistemic coherence). We integrate ECR into a production-grade multi-strategy retrieval pipeline (CSGR++) and analyze its theoretical properties. Our framework provides a rigorous foundation for uncertainty-aware evidence selection, shifting the paradigm from retrieving what is most relevant to retrieving what is most discriminative.

</details>


### [351] [T-Norm Operators for EU AI Act Compliance Classification: An Empirical Comparison of Lukasiewicz, Product, and Gödel Semantics in a Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28558)
*Adam Laabs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 比较三种t-范数算子（Lukasiewicz、Product、Gödel）在欧盟AI法案合规分类的神经符号推理系统中的表现，发现Gödel算子准确率最高但会产生假阳性，而其他算子保持零假阳性但召回率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同逻辑连接机制（t-范数算子）在神经符号推理系统中的应用效果，特别是在欧盟AI法案合规分类这一实际场景中，为选择合适的逻辑算子提供实证依据。

Method: 使用LGGT+引擎和包含1035个标注的AI系统描述（涵盖四个风险类别）的基准数据集，比较Lukasiewicz、Product和Gödel三种t-范数算子在分类准确性、假阳性/假阴性率以及对模糊案例处理行为上的差异。

Result: 三种算子在n=1035时差异显著（McNemar p<0.001）。Gödel算子准确率最高（84.5%），边界案例召回率最佳（85%），但会产生0.8%的假阳性；Lukasiewicz和Product算子保持零假阳性，Product算子表现优于Lukasiewicz（81.2% vs. 78.5%）。

Conclusion: 算子选择的重要性次于规则库的完整性；Lukasiewicz和Product算子能保持零假阳性但会遗漏边界案例；Gödel算子的最小语义能获得更高召回率但需承担假阳性成本；下一步应开发混合语义分类器。研究开源了LGGT+核心引擎和基准数据集。

Abstract: We present a first comparative pilot study of three t-norm operators -- Lukasiewicz (T_L), Product (T_P), and Gödel (T_G) - as logical conjunction mechanisms in a neuro-symbolic reasoning system for EU AI Act compliance classification. Using the LGGT+ (Logic-Guided Graph Transformers Plus) engine and a benchmark of 1035 annotated AI system descriptions spanning four risk categories (prohibited, high_risk, limited_risk, minimal_risk), we evaluate classification accuracy, false positive and false negative rates, and operator behaviour on ambiguous cases. At n=1035, all three operators differ significantly (McNemar p<0.001). T_G achieves highest accuracy (84.5%) and best borderline recall (85%), but introduces 8 false positives (0.8%) via min-semantics over-classification. T_L and T_P maintain zero false positives, with T_P outperforming T_L (81.2% vs. 78.5%). Our principal findings are: (1) operator choice is secondary to rule base completeness; (2) T_L and T_P maintain zero false positives but miss borderline cases; (3) T_G's min-semantics achieves higher recall at cost of 0.8% false positive rate; (4) a mixed-semantics classifier is the productive next step. We release the LGGT+ core engine (201/201 tests passing) and benchmark dataset (n=1035) under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [352] [Towards a Medical AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28589)
*Hongtao Wu,Boyun Zheng,Dingjie Song,Yu Jiang,Jianfeng Gao,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Yixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个面向临床研究的自主AI科学家框架，通过临床-工程协同推理机制生成基于医学证据的研究想法，支持三种研究模式，在171个案例中生成的想法质量显著优于商业LLM，生成的论文接近MICCAI水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统大多领域无关，难以应用于需要医学证据支撑和特殊数据模态的临床医学研究。需要开发专门针对临床研究的自主研究框架。

Method: 提出Medical AI Scientist框架，包含：1）临床-工程协同推理机制，将文献调查转化为可操作的证据；2）基于结构化医学写作规范和伦理政策的证据驱动论文撰写；3）三种研究模式：基于论文的复制、文献启发的创新、任务驱动的探索。

Result: 在171个案例、19个临床任务、6种数据模态上，系统生成的研究想法质量显著优于商业LLM。实验执行成功率更高，方法设计与实现一致性更强。双盲评估显示生成的论文接近MICCAI质量水平，一致优于ISBI和BIBM。

Conclusion: Medical AI Scientist展示了AI在医疗领域自主科学发现的潜力，为临床研究提供了专门化的自主研究框架，能够生成高质量、基于证据的研究想法和接近顶级会议水平的论文。

Abstract: Autonomous systems that generate scientific hypotheses, conduct experiments, and draft manuscripts have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for accelerating discovery. However, existing AI Scientists remain largely domain-agnostic, limiting their applicability to clinical medicine, where research is required to be grounded in medical evidence with specialized data modalities. In this work, we introduce Medical AI Scientist, the first autonomous research framework tailored to clinical autonomous research. It enables clinically grounded ideation by transforming extensively surveyed literature into actionable evidence through clinician-engineer co-reasoning mechanism, which improves the traceability of generated research ideas. It further facilitates evidence-grounded manuscript drafting guided by structured medical compositional conventions and ethical policies. The framework operates under 3 research modes, namely paper-based reproduction, literature-inspired innovation, and task-driven exploration, each corresponding to a distinct level of automated scientific inquiry with progressively increasing autonomy. Comprehensive evaluations by both large language models and human experts demonstrate that the ideas generated by the Medical AI Scientist are of substantially higher quality than those produced by commercial LLMs across 171 cases, 19 clinical tasks, and 6 data modalities. Meanwhile, our system achieves strong alignment between the proposed method and its implementation, while also demonstrating significantly higher success rates in executable experiments. Double-blind evaluations by human experts and the Stanford Agentic Reviewer suggest that the generated manuscripts approach MICCAI-level quality, while consistently surpassing those from ISBI and BIBM. The proposed Medical AI Scientist highlights the potential of leveraging AI for autonomous scientific discovery in healthcare.

</details>


### [353] [MonitorBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28590)
*Han Wang,Yifan Sun,Brian Ko,Mann Talati,Jiawen Gong,Zimeng Li,Naicheng Yu,Xucheng Yu,Wei Shen,Vedant Jolly,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MonitorBench：首个全面开源基准，用于评估大语言模型思维链可监控性，包含1514个测试实例和压力测试设置，发现模型能力与可监控性呈负相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏全面开源基准来研究思维链可监控性问题，当思维链与最终输出因果不匹配时，思维链无法忠实反映模型决策关键因素，导致可监控性降低

Method: 提出MonitorBench基准，包含：(1) 1514个测试实例，涵盖7个类别19个任务，精心设计决策关键因素；(2) 两个压力测试设置，量化可监控性降低程度

Result: 实验发现：需要结构推理时思维链可监控性更高；闭源模型可监控性普遍较低；模型能力与可监控性呈负相关；压力测试下可监控性最多下降30%

Conclusion: MonitorBench为评估未来LLM、研究高级压力测试技术和开发新监控方法提供了基础，揭示了思维链监控的局限性及其与模型能力的关系

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate chains of thought (CoTs) that are not always causally responsible for their final outputs. When such a mismatch occurs, the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the decision-critical factors driving the model's behavior, leading to the reduced CoT monitorability problem. However, a comprehensive and fully open-source benchmark for studying CoT monitorability remains lacking. To address this gap, we propose MonitorBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating CoT monitorability in LLMs. MonitorBench provides: (1) a diverse set of 1,514 test instances with carefully designed decision-critical factors across 19 tasks spanning 7 categories to characterize when CoTs can be used to monitor the factors driving LLM behavior; and (2) two stress-test settings to quantify the extent to which CoT monitorability can be degraded. Extensive experiments across multiple popular LLMs with varying capabilities show that CoT monitorability is higher when producing the final target response requires structural reasoning through the decision-critical factor. Closed-source LLMs generally show lower monitorability, and there exists a negative relationship between monitorability and model capability. Moreover, both open- and closed-source LLMs can intentionally reduce monitorability under stress-tests, with monitorability dropping by up to 30% in some tasks that do not require structural reasoning over the decision-critical factors. Beyond these empirical insights, MonitorBench provides a basis for further research on evaluating future LLMs, studying advanced stress-test monitorability techniques, and developing new monitoring approaches.

</details>


### [354] [Seeing with You: Perception-Reasoning Coevolution for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28618)
*Ziqi Miao,Haonan Jia,Lijun Li,Chen Qian,Yuan Xiong,Wenting Yan,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRCO提出了一种双角色强化学习框架，通过分离感知和推理的奖励信号，解决多模态大语言模型中视觉证据提取的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法通常采用结果驱动的优化策略，使用基于最终答案的共享奖励来同时更新感知和推理模块。这种共享奖励模糊了信用分配，经常改善了推理模式，但未能可靠地提升上游视觉证据提取的准确性。

Method: PRCO是一个双角色RLVR框架，包含两个协作角色：Observer生成针对问题的证据描述，Solver基于该描述预测最终答案。关键创新在于使用角色特定的奖励信号：Solver使用基于最终答案的可验证结果奖励进行优化，而Observer则从Solver的下游成功中获取效用奖励。

Result: 在八个具有挑战性的多模态推理基准测试中，PRCO相比基础模型平均准确率提升了超过7个百分点，优于先前开源的RL调优基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过分离感知和推理的奖励信号，PRCO能够更有效地提升多模态大语言模型的视觉证据提取能力，从而显著改善整体推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially enhanced the reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing RLVR approaches typically rely on outcome-driven optimization that updates both perception and reasoning using a shared reward based solely on the final answer. This shared reward blurs credit assignment, frequently improving reasoning patterns while failing to reliably enhance the accuracy of upstream visual evidence extraction. To address this perception bottleneck, we introduce PRCO (Perception-Reasoning Coevolution), a dual-role RLVR framework with a shared policy. PRCO consists of two cooperative roles: an Observer that generates an evidence caption tailored to the question and a Solver that predicts the final answer based on this caption. Crucially, PRCO employs role-specific reward signals: the Solver is optimized using verifiable outcome rewards on the final answer, while the Observer receives a utility reward derived from the Solver's downstream success. Extensive experiments across eight challenging multimodal reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PRCO yields consistent improvements across model scales by over 7 points on average accuracy compared to the base model, outperforming prior open-source RL-tuned baselines.

</details>


### [355] [The Ultimate Tutorial for AI-driven Scale Development in Generative Psychometrics: Releasing AIGENIE from its Bottle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28643)
*Lara Russell-Lasalandra,Hudson Golino,Luis Eduardo Garrido,Alexander P. Christensen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIGENIE R包实现了AI-GENIE框架，通过大语言模型自动生成心理测量项目，结合网络心理测量方法自动化心理量表开发的早期阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心理量表开发需要大量专家参与、迭代修订和大规模预测试，过程耗时耗力。AIGENIE旨在通过AI技术自动化这一过程的早期阶段，提高效率。

Method: 使用LLM生成候选项目池，将其转换为高维嵌入，然后应用多步降维流程：探索性图分析(EGA)、唯一变量分析(UVA)和bootstrap EGA，完全在计算机中实现结构验证。

Result: 开发了AIGENIE R包，支持多种LLM提供商（OpenAI、Anthropic、Groq、HuggingFace和本地模型），提供完全离线模式，并包含GENIE函数用于现有项目池的心理测量降维。

Conclusion: AIGENIE包为心理测量研究者提供了自动化项目生成和验证的工具，既适用于成熟构念（如大五人格），也适用于新兴构念（如AI焦虑），可显著提高量表开发效率。

Abstract: Psychological scale development has traditionally required extensive expert involvement, iterative revision, and large-scale pilot testing before psychometric evaluation can begin. The `AIGENIE` R package implements the AI-GENIE framework (Automatic Item Generation with Network-Integrated Evaluation), which integrates large language model (LLM) text generation with network psychometric methods to automate the early stages of this process. The package generates candidate item pools using LLMs, transforms them into high-dimensional embeddings, and applies a multi-step reduction pipeline -- Exploratory Graph Analysis (EGA), Unique Variable Analysis (UVA), and bootstrap EGA -- to produce structurally validated item pools entirely *in silico*. This tutorial introduces the package across six parts: installation and setup, understanding Application Programming Interfaces (APIs), text generation, item generation, the `AIGENIE` function, and the `GENIE` function. Two running examples illustrate the package's use: the Big Five personality model (a well-established construct) and AI Anxiety (an emerging construct). The package supports multiple LLM providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Groq, HuggingFace, and local models), offers a fully offline mode with no external API calls, and provides the `GENIE()` function for researchers who wish to apply the psychometric reduction pipeline to existing item pools regardless of their origin. The `AIGENIE` package is freely available on R-universe at https://laralee.r-universe.dev/AIGENIE.

</details>


### [356] [Not Search, But Scan: Benchmarking MLLMs on Scan-Oriented Academic Paper Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28651)
*Rongjin Li,Zichen Tang,Xianghe Wang,Xinyi Hu,Zhengyu Wang,Zhengyu Lu,Yiling Huang,Jiayuan Chen,Weisheng Tan,Jiacheng Liu,Zhongjun Yang,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScholScan是一个新的学术论文推理基准，采用扫描导向的任务范式，要求模型像人类研究者一样阅读和交叉检查整篇论文，识别一致性错误，包含1800个标注问题，涵盖9个错误类别和13个自然科学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在学术论文推理方面主要局限于以搜索为导向的范式，基于相关性检索进行推理，难以支持研究者式的全文理解、推理和验证。需要新的基准来评估模型在扫描导向任务上的能力。

Method: 提出ScholScan基准，采用扫描导向的任务设置，要求模型阅读和交叉检查整篇论文来识别一致性错误。包含1800个精心标注的问题，涵盖9个错误类别、13个自然科学领域和715篇论文，提供证据定位和推理轨迹的详细标注，以及统一的评估协议。

Result: 评估了15个模型在24种输入配置下的表现，检索增强生成方法没有显著改进，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在扫描导向任务上的系统性缺陷，突显了ScholScan带来的挑战。

Conclusion: ScholScan代表了扫描导向任务范式的领先工作，为评估模型在学术论文全文理解和推理能力方面提供了新的基准，揭示了当前模型的局限性，并有望推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: With the rapid progress of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), AI already performs well at literature retrieval and certain reasoning tasks, serving as a capable assistant to human researchers, yet it remains far from autonomous research. The fundamental reason is that current work on academic paper reasoning is largely confined to a search-oriented paradigm centered on pre-specified targets, with reasoning grounded in relevance retrieval, which struggles to support researcher-style full-document understanding, reasoning, and verification. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{ScholScan}, a new benchmark for academic paper reasoning. ScholScan introduces a scan-oriented task setting that asks models to read and cross-check entire papers like human researchers, scanning the document to identify consistency issues. The benchmark comprises 1,800 carefully annotated questions drawn from nine error categories across 13 natural-science domains and 715 papers, and provides detailed annotations for evidence localization and reasoning traces, together with a unified evaluation protocol. We assessed 15 models across 24 input configurations and conducted a fine-grained analysis of MLLM capabilities for all error categories. Across the board, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods yield no significant improvements, revealing systematic deficiencies of current MLLMs on scan-oriented tasks and underscoring the challenge posed by ScholScan. We expect ScholScan to be the leading and representative work of the scan-oriented task paradigm.

</details>


### [357] [Dynamic Dual-Granularity Skill Bank for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28716)
*Songjun Tu,Chengdong Xu,Qichao Zhang,Yaocheng Zhang,Xiangyuan Lan,Linjing Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D2Skill：为智能体强化学习设计的动态双粒度技能库，通过任务技能和步骤技能组织可重用经验，显著提升成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的方法主要提取轨迹级指导，缺乏维护演化技能库的原则性机制，智能体强化学习需要更好的可重用经验利用方式

Method: 提出动态双粒度技能库D2Skill，包含任务技能（高层指导）和步骤技能（细粒度决策支持）。通过配对基线和技能注入rollouts联合训练策略和技能库，利用性能差距生成后见之明效用信号。技能库通过反思持续扩展，并采用效用感知的检索和剪枝维护

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型，D2Skill相比无技能基线持续提升成功率10-20个百分点。双粒度技能建模和动态技能维护对性能提升至关重要

Conclusion: D2Skill通过动态双粒度技能库有效组织可重用经验，显著提升智能体强化学习性能，学习到的技能具有更高效用、可跨评估设置迁移，且训练开销适中

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) can benefit substantially from reusable experience, yet existing skill-based methods mainly extract trajectory-level guidance and often lack principled mechanisms for maintaining an evolving skill memory. We propose D2Skill, a dynamic dual-granularity skill bank for agentic RL that organizes reusable experience into task skills for high-level guidance and step skills for fine-grained decision support and error correction. D2Skill jointly trains the policy and skill bank through paired baseline and skill-injected rollouts under the same policy, using their performance gap to derive hindsight utility signals for both skill updating and policy optimization. Built entirely from training-time experience, the skill bank is continuously expanded through reflection and maintained with utility-aware retrieval and pruning. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 show that D2Skill consistently improves success rates over skill-free baselines by 10-20 points. Further ablations and analyses show that both dual-granularity skill modeling and dynamic skill maintenance are critical to these gains, while the learned skills exhibit higher utility, transfer across evaluation settings, and introduce only modest training overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [358] [Co-designing a Social Robot for Newcomer Children's Cultural and Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26674)
*Neil Fernandes,Tehniyat Shahbaz,Emily Davies-Robinson,Yue Hu,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过协同设计探索社交机器人Maple在移民儿童语言学习中的应用，总结领域挑战、文化归属、机器人角色及初步设计指南


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新移民儿童在获取东道国语言方面面临障碍，而现有扫盲项目受限于人员不足、混合能力班级和有限接触时间。社交辅助机器人(SARs)在教育中显示出潜力，但在这些社会情感敏感环境中的应用仍未被充分探索。

Method: 采用协同设计研究，与项目导师和协调员合作，探索社交机器人Maple的设计空间。通过专家访谈和讨论收集数据。

Result: 贡献包括：(1)总结四个反复出现的挑战的领域概述；(2)关于文化导向和社区归属与机器人的讨论；(3)专家基础的SAR在文化和语言学习中的角色认知讨论；(4)将SAR整合到课堂的初步设计指南。

Conclusion: 这些基于专家的见解为与新移民儿童及其家庭的迭代设计和评估奠定了基础，为社交机器人在敏感教育环境中的应用提供了理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: Newcomer children face barriers in acquiring the host country's language and literacy programs are often constrained by limited staffing, mixed-proficiency cohorts, and short contact time. While Socially Assistive Robots (SARs) show promise in education, their use in these socio-emotionally sensitive settings remains underexplored. This research presents a co-design study with program tutors and coordinators, to explore the design space for a social robot, Maple. We contribute (1) a domain summary outlining four recurring challenges, (2) a discussion on cultural orientation and community belonging with robots, (3) an expert-grounded discussion of the perceived role of an SAR in cultural and language learning, and (4) preliminary design guidelines for integrating an SAR into a classroom. These expert-grounded insights lay the foundation for iterative design and evaluation with newcomer children and their families.

</details>


### [359] [Contextual Graph Representations for Task-Driven 3D Perception and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26685)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何利用图神经网络从3D场景图中学习高效的任务规划表示，以解决现有方法在资源受限环境中的可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图虽然能自动提取物体中心的关系表示，但包含过多对象和关系，导致任务规划状态空间过大，无法在资源受限的机器人系统中部署。

Method: 1) 评估现有具身AI环境对机器人任务规划与3D场景图交叉研究的适用性；2) 构建基准测试用于经典规划器的经验比较；3) 探索使用图神经网络利用规划领域关系结构中的不变性，学习支持更快规划的表示。

Result: 论文构建了用于比较最先进经典规划器的基准测试，并探索了图神经网络在利用关系结构不变性、学习高效规划表示方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过图神经网络学习3D场景图的紧凑表示可以显著提高机器人任务规划的效率，特别是在资源受限的环境中，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in computer vision facilitate fully automatic extraction of object-centric relational representations from visual-inertial data. These state representations, dubbed 3D scene graphs, are a hierarchical decomposition of real-world scenes with a dense multiplex graph structure. While 3D scene graphs claim to promote efficient task planning for robot systems, they contain numerous objects and relations when only small subsets are required for a given task. This magnifies the state space that task planners must operate over and prohibits deployment in resource constrained settings. This thesis tests the suitability of existing embodied AI environments for research at the intersection of robot task planning and 3D scene graphs and constructs a benchmark for empirical comparison of state-of-the-art classical planners. Furthermore, we explore the use of graph neural networks to harness invariances in the relational structure of planning domains and learn representations that afford faster planning.

</details>


### [360] [Bridging the Awareness Gap: Socially Mediated State Externalization for Transparent Distributed Home Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26686)
*Wenzheng Zhao,Manideep Duggi,Fengpei Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个社交中介机器人系统，通过实时状态外化来弥合用户与隐藏机器人之间的感知差距，显著提升了用户体验而不影响任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭分布式多机器人系统经常需要在用户视线外操作，这造成了状态感知差距，降低了用户的信任感、透明度和控制感。研究旨在探索是否可以通过实时社交中介状态外化来弥合这一差距。

Method: 开发了一个系统，其中社交中介机器人Pepper通过语音更新和视觉进度显示，实时外化隐藏的移动机械臂Stretch~3在执行语音驱动物体取送任务时的状态。采用30人参与的反平衡组内研究设计，比较了"自主隐藏执行"基线条件和"社交中介状态外化"条件。

Result: 状态外化显著提高了用户任务关注度（从15.8%提升到84.6%，p<.001），显著改善了感知清晰度、可靠性、刺激性和吸引力（所有p<.001）。83%的参与者偏好外化条件，且用户体验的提升没有显著增加端到端任务完成时间（p=.271）。

Conclusion: 社交中介状态外化是设计更透明、更可信赖的分布式机器人系统的有效架构机制，能够在分布式家庭机器人部署中提升用户体验而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Distributed multi-robot systems for the home often require robots to operate out of the user's sight, creating a state awareness gap that can diminish trust and perceived transparency and control. This paper investigates whether real-time, socially mediated state externalization can bridge this gap without compromising task performance. We developed a system where a co-located social mediator robot (Pepper) externalizes the hidden execution states of an out-of-sight mobile manipulator (Stretch~3) for voice-driven object retrieval and delivery, where task-level states are synchronized and externalized through verbal updates and visual progress display. In a counterbalanced within-subject study (N=30), we compared a baseline of Autonomous Hidden Execution against Socially Mediated State Externalization. Our results show that externalization significantly increases user task-focused attention (from 15.8% to 84.6%, p<.001) and substantially improves perceived perspicuity, dependability, stimulation, and attractiveness (all p<.001). Furthermore, 83% of participants preferred the externalized condition, and this improvement in user experience was achieved without a statistically significant increase in end-to-end task completion time (p=.271). The results suggest that socially mediated state externalization is an effective architectural mechanism for designing more transparent and trustworthy distributed robot systems, ultimately enhancing user experience without sacrificing performance in distributed home robot deployments.

</details>


### [361] [Learning Energy-Efficient Air--Ground Actuation for Hybrid Robots on Stair-Like Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26687)
*Jiaxing Li,Wen Tian,Xinhang Xu,Junbin Yuan,Sebastian Scherer,Muqing Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于能量感知强化学习的混合空中-地面机器人控制框架，通过单一连续策略协调螺旋桨、轮子和倾斜舵机，实现推力辅助驾驶，相比传统方法显著降低能耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合空中-地面机器人在楼梯状不连续地形上面临权衡：仅用轮子容易在边缘卡住，而飞行模式对小高度增益能耗过高。需要一种能协调多种执行器的高效控制方法

Method: 提出能量感知强化学习框架，训练单一连续策略协调螺旋桨、轮子和倾斜舵机，不使用预定义的空中和地面模式。在Isaac Lab中使用并行环境训练，基于硬件校准的推力/功率模型，奖励函数惩罚真实电能消耗

Result: 学习到的策略发现了推力辅助驾驶，混合空中推力和地面牵引。仿真中能耗比仅用螺旋桨控制低约4倍。在DoubleBee原型机上8cm间隙攀爬任务中，平均功率比基于规则的解耦控制器低38%

Conclusion: 高效混合驱动可以通过学习自然产生并部署到硬件上，证明了能量感知强化学习框架在协调多种执行器方面的有效性

Abstract: Hybrid aerial--ground robots offer both traversability and endurance, but stair-like discontinuities create a trade-off: wheels alone often stall at edges, while flight is energy-hungry for small height gains. We propose an energy-aware reinforcement learning framework that trains a single continuous policy to coordinate propellers, wheels, and tilt servos without predefined aerial and ground modes. We train policies from proprioception and a local height scan in Isaac Lab with parallel environments, using hardware-calibrated thrust/power models so the reward penalizes true electrical energy. The learned policy discovers thrust-assisted driving that blends aerial thrust and ground traction. In simulation it achieves about 4 times lower energy than propeller-only control. We transfer the policy to a DoubleBee prototype on an 8cm gap-climbing task; it achieves 38% lower average power than a rule-based decoupled controller. These results show that efficient hybrid actuation can emerge from learning and deploy on hardware.

</details>


### [362] [SpatialPoint: Spatial-aware Point Prediction for Embodied Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26690)
*Qiming Zhu,Zhirui Fang,Tianming Zhang,Chuanxiu Liu,Xiaoke Jiang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpatialPoint框架，将深度信息整合到视觉语言模型中，解决具身定位问题，预测可执行的3D点坐标，并在真实机器人任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能需要确定在3D空间中的操作位置，现有视觉语言系统主要依赖RGB输入，缺乏显式几何信息，限制了跨场景泛化能力，尽管机器人领域广泛使用RGB-D传感器。

Method: 提出SpatialPoint框架，将结构化深度信息整合到视觉语言模型中，生成相机坐标系下的3D坐标。构建包含260万样本的RGB-D数据集，涵盖可触摸点和空中点两种目标类型。

Result: 实验表明，将深度信息整合到VLM中显著提升了具身定位性能。在三个代表性机器人任务中成功验证：语言引导的机械臂抓取、物体放置到目标位置、移动机器人导航。

Conclusion: SpatialPoint通过整合深度信息有效解决了具身定位问题，为具身智能提供了更好的3D空间推理能力，并在真实机器人任务中展示了实用性。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence fundamentally requires a capability to determine where to act in 3D space. We formalize this requirement as embodied localization -- the problem of predicting executable 3D points conditioned on visual observations and language instructions. We instantiate embodied localization with two complementary target types: touchable points, surface-grounded 3D points enabling direct physical interaction, and air points, free-space 3D points specifying placement and navigation goals, directional constraints, or geometric relations. Embodied localization is inherently a problem of embodied 3D spatial reasoning -- yet most existing vision-language systems rely predominantly on RGB inputs, necessitating implicit geometric reconstruction that limits cross-scene generalization, despite the widespread adoption of RGB-D sensors in robotics. To address this gap, we propose SpatialPoint, a spatial-aware vision-language framework with careful design that integrates structured depth into a vision-language model (VLM) and generates camera-frame 3D coordinates. We construct a 2.6M-sample RGB-D dataset covering both touchable and air points QA pairs for training and evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that incorporating depth into VLMs significantly improves embodied localization performance. We further validate SpatialPoint through real-robot deployment across three representative tasks: language-guided robotic arm grasping at specified locations, object placement to target destinations, and mobile robot navigation to goal positions.

</details>


### [363] [Topological Motion Planning Diffusion: Generative Tangle-Free Path Planning for Tethered Robots in Obstacle-Rich Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26696)
*Yifu Tian,Xinhang Xu,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Muqing Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TMPD框架，结合拓扑记忆和扩散模型，解决系留机器人在复杂环境中的终身运动规划问题，避免电缆缠绕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在水下探索和灾后救援等极端环境中，系留机器人需要连续导航同时避免电缆缠绕。传统规划器在终身规划场景中因缺乏拓扑意识而表现不佳，而拓扑增强的图搜索方法在障碍物丰富的环境中面临计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出拓扑运动规划扩散(TMPD)框架：1) 前端使用扩散模型生成多模态、运动学可行的轨迹候选，覆盖不同同伦类；2) 后端通过计算广义卷绕数评估拓扑能量，筛选和优化候选轨迹。

Result: 在障碍物丰富的模拟环境中，TMPD达到100%无碰撞到达率和97.0%无缠绕率，在几何平滑性和计算效率上优于传统拓扑搜索和纯运动学扩散基线方法。

Conclusion: TMPD通过结合终身拓扑记忆和生成式规划，有效解决了系留机器人在复杂环境中的运动规划问题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In extreme environments such as underwater exploration and post-disaster rescue, tethered robots require continuous navigation while avoiding cable entanglement. Traditional planners struggle in these lifelong planning scenarios due to topological unawareness, while topology-augmented graph-search methods face computational bottlenecks in obstacle-rich environments where the number of candidate topological classes increases. To address these challenges, we propose Topological Motion Planning Diffusion (TMPD), a novel generative planning framework that integrates lifelong topological memory. Instead of relying on sequential graph search, TMPD leverages a diffusion model to propose a multimodal front-end of kinematically feasible trajectory candidates across various homotopy classes. A tether-aware topological back-end then filters and optimizes these candidates by computing generalized winding numbers to evaluate their topological energy against the accumulated tether configuration. Benchmarking in obstacle-rich simulated environments demonstrates that TMPD achieves a collision-free reach of 100% and a tangle-free rate of 97.0%, outperforming traditional topological search and purely kinematic diffusion baselines in both geometric smoothness and computational efficiency. Simulation with realistic cable dynamics further validates the practicality of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [364] [Surface-Constrained Offline Warping with Contact-Aware Online Pose Projection for Safe Robotic Trajectory Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26711)
*Farong Wang,Sai Swaminathan,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架，将离线几何嵌入与在线执行调节分离，实现曲面上的稳定重复轨迹执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人表面处理任务中，将设计好的运动基元直接平铺到非平面曲面会导致几何不一致、穿透、方向不连续和累积漂移等问题

Method: 1) 离线表面约束变形算子：通过非对称微分同胚变形和轴一致方向补全，将周期性基元嵌入曲面；2) 在线接触感知投影算子：使用FSR驱动扰动适应和圆锥方向安全约束，确保相对于参考轨迹的有界偏差

Result: 在多个解析曲面族和真实机器人正弦曲面验证中，相比直接平铺方法，改善了几何连续性，减少了大幅方向跳跃，实现了鲁棒的接触保持

Conclusion: 将离线几何重映射与轻量级在线投影解耦，能够在传感器有限反馈下实现稳定可重复的表面嵌入轨迹执行

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks that require repeated tool motion along curved surfaces frequently arise in surface finishing, inspection, and guided interaction. In practice, nominal motion primitives are often designed independently of the deployment surface and later reused across varying geometries. Directly tiling such primitives onto nonplanar surfaces introduces geometric inconsistencies, leading to interpenetration, orientation discontinuities, and cumulative drift over repeated cycles. We present a two-stage framework that separates geometric embedding from execution-level regulation. An offline surface-constrained warping operator embeds a nominal periodic primitive onto curved surfaces through asymmetric diffeomorphic deformation of dual-track waypoints and axis-consistent orientation completion, producing a surface-adapted reference trajectory. An online contact-aware projection operator then enforces bounded deviation relative to this reference using FSR-driven disturbance adaptation and a conic orientation safety constraint. Experiments across multiple analytic surface families and real-robot validation on a sinusoidal surface demonstrate improved geometric continuity, reduced large orientation jumps, and robust contact maintenance compared with direct tiling. These results show that decoupling offline geometric remapping from lightweight online projection enables stable and repeatable surface-embedded trajectory execution under sensor-lite feedbacks.

</details>


### [365] [SutureAgent: Learning Surgical Trajectories via Goal-conditioned Offline RL in Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26720)
*Huanrong Liu,Chunlin Tian,Tongyu Jia,Tailai Zhou,Qin Liu,Yu Gao,Yutong Ban,Yun Gu,Guy Rosman,Xin Ma,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SutureAgent框架，将内窥镜视频中的缝合针轨迹预测建模为像素空间中的顺序决策问题，采用离线强化学习结合稀疏标注，在肾脏伤口缝合数据集上实现58.6%的误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接从视觉观察学习运动分布时忽略了相邻运动步骤之间的顺序依赖关系，且稀疏的路径点标注无法提供足够的监督信号，增加了监督学习或模仿学习的难度。

Method: 将针尖视为在像素空间中逐步移动的智能体，采用目标条件离线强化学习框架，通过三次样条插值将稀疏标注转化为密集奖励信号，使用观察编码器捕捉局部空间线索和长期时间动态，并通过离散方向与连续幅度组合的动作自回归预测未来路径点。

Result: 在包含50名患者1,158条轨迹的肾脏伤口缝合数据集上，SutureAgent相比最强基线将平均位移误差降低了58.6%，证明了将针轨迹预测建模为像素级顺序动作学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 将图像引导的针轨迹预测建模为顺序决策问题，结合离线强化学习和稀疏标注，能够有效捕捉针运动的连续性并建模物理合理的像素级状态转移，显著提升预测精度。

Abstract: Predicting surgical needle trajectories from endoscopic video is critical for robot-assisted suturing, enabling anticipatory planning, real-time guidance, and safer motion execution. Existing methods that directly learn motion distributions from visual observations tend to overlook the sequential dependency among adjacent motion steps. Moreover, sparse waypoint annotations often fail to provide sufficient supervision, further increasing the difficulty of supervised or imitation learning methods. To address these challenges, we formulate image-based needle trajectory prediction as a sequential decision-making problem, in which the needle tip is treated as an agent that moves step by step in pixel space. This formulation naturally captures the continuity of needle motion and enables the explicit modeling of physically plausible pixel-wise state transitions over time. From this perspective, we propose SutureAgent, a goal-conditioned offline reinforcement learning framework that leverages sparse annotations to dense reward signals via cubic spline interpolation, encouraging the policy to exploit limited expert guidance while exploring plausible future motion paths. SutureAgent encodes variable-length clips using an observation encoder to capture both local spatial cues and long-range temporal dynamics, and autoregressively predicts future waypoints through actions composed of discrete directions and continuous magnitudes. To enable stable offline policy optimization from expert demonstrations, we adopt Conservative Q-Learning with Behavioral Cloning regularization. Experiments on a new kidney wound suturing dataset containing 1,158 trajectories from 50 patients show that SutureAgent reduces Average Displacement Error by 58.6% compared with the strongest baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of modeling needle trajectory prediction as pixel-level sequential action learning.

</details>


### [366] [Why Cognitive Robotics Matters: Lessons from OntoAgent and LLM Deployment in HARMONIC for Safety-Critical Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26730)
*Sanjay Oruganti,Sergei Nirenburg,Marjorie McShane,Jesse English,Michael Roberts,Christian Arndt,Ramviyas Parasuraman,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HARMONIC架构结合OntoAgent认知架构与反应式战术层，评估LLM能否替代OntoAgent的认知能力，发现LLM在知识状态评估、诊断推理和动作选择方面存在缺陷，支持认知架构在物理具身系统中保持主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界中部署具身AI代理需要具备长时程规划能力，且执行需可靠、确定和透明。需要评估大型语言模型(LLM)是否能够替代传统认知架构的认知能力。

Method: 提出HARMONIC认知-机器人架构，将OntoAgent（基于本体知识结构的认知架构）与模块化反应式战术层配对。在相同机器人系统和条件下，用6个前沿和高效层级的LLM替代OntoAgent，在协作维护场景中评估其认知能力。

Result: LLM在行动前不能一致地评估自身知识状态，导致诊断推理和动作选择的下游失败。即使在拥有同等程序知识的情况下，这些缺陷仍然存在，表明问题是架构性的而非知识性的。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在物理具身系统中，认知架构应保持主要推理权威，因其具有确定性和透明性特征。LLM目前无法完全替代传统认知架构的核心认知能力。

Abstract: Deploying embodied AI agents in the physical world demands cognitive capabilities for long-horizon planning that execute reliably, deterministically, and transparently. We present HARMONIC, a cognitive-robotic architecture that pairs OntoAgent, a content-centric cognitive architecture providing metacognitive self-monitoring, domain-grounded diagnosis, and consequence-based action selection over ontologically structured knowledge, with a modular reactive tactical layer. HARMONIC's modular design enables a functional evaluation of whether LLMs can replicate OntoAgent's cognitive capabilities, evaluated within the same robotic system under identical conditions. Six LLMs spanning frontier and efficient tiers replace OntoAgent in a collaborative maintenance scenario under native and knowledge-equalized conditions. Results reveal that LLMs do not consistently assess their own knowledge state before acting, causing downstream failures in diagnostic reasoning and action selection. These deficits persist even with equivalent procedural knowledge, indicating the issues are architectural rather than knowledge-based. These findings support the design of physically embodied systems in which cognitive architectures retain primary authority for reasoning, owing to their deterministic and transparent characteristics.

</details>


### [367] [Motion as a Sensing Modality for Metric Scale in Monocular Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26740)
*Hadush Hailu,Bruk Gebregziabher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过轨迹设计改善单目视觉惯性里程计（VIO）的尺度恢复：理论分析表明平移加速度（由曲率产生）是尺度与惯性状态耦合的关键，提出轻量级激励度量，实验验证轨迹设计可显著提升尺度恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目VIO无法仅从视觉恢复度量尺度，必须通过惯性测量解决尺度问题。传统方法未充分理解轨迹运动如何影响尺度可观测性，需要建立轨迹设计与尺度恢复之间的理论联系。

Method: 1）进行轨迹相关的可观测性分析，揭示平移加速度（由曲率产生）是尺度与惯性状态耦合的基本来源；2）基于IMU模型中的重力-加速度不对称性形式化这一关系；3）推导可观测性矩阵的秩条件；4）提出可从原始IMU数据计算的轻量级激励度量。

Result: 在差速机器人上使用单目相机和消费级IMU进行控制实验：直线运动产生9.2%尺度误差，圆周运动6.4%，八字形运动4.8%，激励度量跨越四个数量级。验证了轨迹设计对改善尺度恢复的有效性。

Conclusion: 轨迹设计是改善度量尺度恢复的实用机制。曲率产生的平移加速度是尺度可观测性的关键，提出的激励度量为轨迹优化提供了理论基础，有助于提升单目VIO系统的实际性能。

Abstract: Monocular visual-inertial odometry (VIO) cannot recover metric scale from vision alone; scale must be resolved through inertial measurements. We present a trajectory-dependent observability analysis showing that translational acceleration, produced by curvature, not constant-speed straight-line travel, is the fundamental source that couples scale to the inertial state. This relationship is formalized through the gravity-acceleration asymmetry in the IMU model, from which we derive rank conditions on the observability matrix and propose a lightweight excitation metric computable from raw IMU data. Controlled experiments on a differential-drive robot with a monocular camera and consumer-grade IMU validate the theory, with straight-line motion yielding 9.2% scale error, circular motion 6.4%, and figure-eight motion 4.8%, with excitation spanning four orders of magnitude. These results establish trajectory design as a practical mechanism for improving metric scale recovery.

</details>


### [368] [LARD 2.0: Enhanced Datasets and Benchmarking for Autonomous Landing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26748)
*Yassine Bougacha,Geoffrey Delhomme,Mélanie Ducoffe,Augustin Fuchs,Jean-Brice Ginestet,Jacques Girard,Sofiane Kraiem,Franck Mamalet,Vincent Mussot,Claire Pagetti,Thierry Sammour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对自主着陆系统中机器学习模型监督训练的数据集限制问题，通过增强数据集多样性、优化操作设计域以及建立ML模型基准测试框架来解决关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主着陆系统的机器学习模型训练面临数据集限制问题，现有数据集生成器（如LARD）的多样性不足，操作设计域存在不现实的着陆场景，且缺乏多跑道机场覆盖，需要建立系统的ML模型基准测试框架。

Method: 1. 增强数据集多样性：引入BingMap航空图像和飞行模拟器作为新数据源，扩展现有数据集生成器的生成范围；2. 优化操作设计域：解决不现实的着陆场景问题，扩展多跑道机场覆盖；3. 建立基准测试框架：为复杂多实例环境下的目标检测子任务提供评估框架，并开源相关模型作为性能基准。

Result: 提出了一个改进的数据集生成方法，扩展了LARD数据集的多样性；优化了操作设计域使其更符合实际着陆场景；建立了自主着陆系统ML模型的基准测试框架，并提供了开源模型作为性能基准。

Conclusion: 该研究通过增强数据集多样性、优化操作设计域和建立基准测试框架，显著改善了自主着陆系统中机器学习模型的训练和评估，为后续研究提供了重要的基础工具和参考标准。

Abstract: This paper addresses key challenges in the development of autonomous landing systems, focusing on dataset limitations for supervised training of Machine Learning (ML) models for object detection. Our main contributions include: (1) Enhancing dataset diversity, by advocating for the inclusion of new sources such as BingMap aerial images and Flight Simulator, to widen the generation scope of an existing dataset generator used to produce the dataset LARD; (2) Refining the Operational Design Domain (ODD), addressing issues like unrealistic landing scenarios and expanding coverage to multi-runway airports; (3) Benchmarking ML models for autonomous landing systems, introducing a framework for evaluating object detection subtask in a complex multi-instances setting, and providing associated open-source models as a baseline for AI models' performance.

</details>


### [369] [Functionalization of Situated Robots via Vapour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26752)
*Kadri-Ann Pankratov,Leonid Zinatullin,Adele Metsniit,Marie Vihmar,Indrek Must*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用环境材料功能化原位纺丝结构，减少有效载荷并实现环境匹配


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原位构建身体的机器人（如纺丝机器人）需要与环境紧密匹配，但现有功能化方法面临材料限制和集成挑战

Method: 提出利用环境材料功能化原位纺丝结构，通过吡咯蒸气暴露将PVDF纤维网转化为聚吡咯接枝结构，展示两种活化剂递送策略

Result: 成功将光学散射的PVDF纤维网转化为光学吸收的聚吡咯接枝结构，证明环境材料功能化的可行性

Conclusion: 环境材料功能化为原位纺丝机器人提供新途径，减少有效载荷并实现环境匹配，未来可扩展至生物混合机器人等领域

Abstract: Tight matching with the environment is key to effective robot operation in complex settings. Situated robots that build their bodies in situ (e.g. by spinning) are uniquely positioned to exploit their surroundings, yet functionalization of these structures remains an integration challenge - multimaterial spinning requires complex spinneret multiplexing, and mixture doping is limited by additive availability and chemical stability. We propose instead using materials available in the environment to functionalize in situ spun webs, reducing payload and uniquely matching the structure to its surroundings. As a demonstration, we transform an optically scattering PVDF fiber web into an optically absorbing, polypyrrole-grafted structure via pyrrole vapour exposure. Two activator-delivery strategies are shown: liquid infusion into a prefabricated web, and activator pre-embedding in the spinning mixture. Beyond this proof-of-concept, we foresee broader applications including biohybrid robots that exploit bacterial genomes for specific biomolecule synthesis in situ.

</details>


### [370] [Reasoning Systems for Semantic Navigation in Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26753)
*Jonathan Crespo,Ramón Barber,O. M. Mozos,Daniel Beßler,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于本体模型的环境语义表示方法（关系数据库和KnowRob），集成到语义导航系统中，并进行定性和定量比较，最终在移动机器人上实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义导航通过考虑环境语义概念及其关系来规划机器人路径，有助于与人类交互和理解人类环境。需要建立环境语义表示和推理系统，本文专注于开发基于语义概念的环境模型。

Method: 提出两种实现语义导航范式的系统：1）使用关系数据库的本体模型；2）基于KnowRob的本体模型。两种系统都集成到语义导航器中，并进行定性和定量比较。

Result: 成功开发了两种语义导航系统，在移动机器人上实现了概念验证。通过比较分析，展示了两种方法在语义环境建模方面的性能差异和适用场景。

Conclusion: 两种基于本体模型的语义导航系统都能有效实现语义导航，为机器人理解人类环境和与人类交互提供了可行方案。比较分析为不同应用场景下的系统选择提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Semantic navigation is the navigation paradigm in which environmental semantic concepts and their relationships are taken into account to plan the route of a mobile robot. This paradigm facilitates the interaction with humans and the understanding of human environments in terms of navigation goals and tasks. At the high level, a semantic navigation system requires two main components: a semantic representation of the environment, and a reasoner system. This paper is focused on develop a model of the environment using semantic concepts. This paper presents two solutions for the semantic navigation paradigm. Both systems implement an ontological model. Whilst the first one uses a relational database, the second one is based on KnowRob. Both systems have been integrated in a semantic navigator. We compare both systems at the qualitative and quantitative levels, and present an implementation on a mobile robot as a proof of concept.

</details>


### [371] [Beyond Viewpoint Generalization: What Multi-View Demonstrations Offer and How to Synthesize Them for Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26757)
*Boyang Cai,Qiwei Liang,Jiawei Li,Shihang Weng,Zhaoxin Zhang,Tao Lin,Xiangyu Chen,Wenjie Zhang,Jiaqi Mao,Weisheng Xu,Bin Yang,Jiaming Liang,Junhao Cai,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多视角演示确实能提升机器人操作性能，而不仅仅是增强跨视角鲁棒性。研究发现多视角数据能突破单视角数据集的性能瓶颈，并通过RoboNVS框架生成新视角视频来缓解真实世界多视角数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多视角演示对机器人操作的实际价值，量化其性能提升、扩展行为和底层机制。由于大规模机器人数据集中多视角数据稀缺，且真实世界收集额外视角困难，需要开发新方法来生成多视角数据。

Method: 通过控制实验系统研究多视角数据的影响，分析性能与视角覆盖度的关系。提出RoboNVS框架——一种几何感知的自监督框架，能够从单目输入合成新视角视频，用于增强下游策略。

Result: 多视角演示能持续提升单视角策略的成功率和泛化能力，性能与视角覆盖度呈非单调关系。多视角数据能突破单视角数据集的性能瓶颈，在饱和后继续提升性能上限。RoboNVS生成的数据在仿真和真实环境中都能改善下游策略。

Conclusion: 多视角数据确实能实质性提升机器人操作性能，而不仅仅是增强鲁棒性。通过几何感知的自监督方法生成新视角数据是解决真实世界多视角数据稀缺的有效途径，为机器人学习提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Does multi-view demonstration truly improve robot manipulation, or merely enhance cross-view robustness? We present a systematic study quantifying the performance gains, scaling behavior, and underlying mechanisms of multi-view data for robot manipulation. Controlled experiments show that, under both fixed and randomized backgrounds, multi-view demonstrations consistently improve single-view policy success and generalization. Performance varies non-monotonically with view coverage, revealing effective regimes rather than a simple "more is better" trend. Notably, multi-view data breaks the scaling limitation of single-view datasets and continues to raise performance ceilings after saturation. Mechanistic analysis shows that multi-view learning promotes manipulation-relevant visual representations, better aligns the action head with the learned feature distribution, and reduces overfitting. Motivated by the importance of multi-view data and its scarcity in large-scale robotic datasets, as well as the difficulty of collecting additional viewpoints in real world settings, we propose RoboNVS, a geometry-aware self-supervised framework that synthesizes novel-view videos from monocular inputs. The generated data consistently improves downstream policies in both simulation and real-world environments.

</details>


### [372] [Robot Arm Control via Cognitive Map Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26773)
*Nathan McDonald,Colyn Seeley,Christian Brazeau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用认知地图学习器（CML）控制多关节机械臂运动，无需逆运动学方程，通过超向量编码和分解实现目标点定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械臂控制需要复杂的逆运动学方程，该研究旨在探索使用认知地图学习器的分层组合特性来实现更灵活、无需任务特定重训练的机械臂控制方法。

Method: 使用分数幂编码将目标点编码为相量超向量，通过谐振网络或现代Hopfield网络分解为各臂段角度，每个臂段由独立训练的CML模块控制，实现2D平面内任意数量臂段的通用解决方案和3D单旋转基座的特殊解决方案。

Result: 成功实现了无需逆运动学方程的机械臂控制，展示了CML模块的分层组合能力，能够将训练好的模块任意组合解决更复杂问题而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 认知地图学习器为机械臂控制提供了一种新颖的替代方法，展示了分层组合机器学习在机器人控制中的潜力，避免了传统逆运动学计算的复杂性。

Abstract: Cognitive map learners (CML) have been shown to enable hierarchical, compositional machine learning. That is, interpedently trained CML modules can be arbitrarily composed together to solve more complex problems without task-specific retraining. This work applies this approach to control the movement of a multi-jointed robot arm, whereby each arm segment's angular position is governed by an independently trained CML. Operating in a 2D Cartesian plane, target points are encoded as phasor hypervectors according to fractional power encoding (FPE). This phasor hypervector is then factorized into a set of arm segment angles either via a resonator network or a modern Hopfield network. These arm segment angles are subsequently fed to their respective arm segment CMLs, which reposition the robot arm to the target point without the use of inverse kinematic equations. This work presents both a general solution for both a 2D robot arm with an arbitrary number of arm segments and a particular solution for a 3D arm with a single rotating base.

</details>


### [373] [ReMemNav: A Rethinking and Memory-Augmented Framework for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26788)
*Feng Wu,Wei Zuo,Wenliang Yang,Jun Xiao,Yang Liu,Xinhua Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReMemNav：一种结合全景语义先验和情景记忆的层次化导航框架，用于零样本物体导航，显著提升了成功率和探索效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本物体导航要求智能体在陌生环境中定位未见过的目标物体，无需先验地图或任务特定训练，这仍然是一个重大挑战。尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）为此任务提供了有前景的常识推理能力，但这些模型仍存在空间幻觉、局部探索死锁以及高层语义意图与低层控制之间的脱节问题。

Method: 提出ReMemNav层次化导航框架，整合全景语义先验和情景记忆与VLMs。引入Recognize Anything Model来锚定VLM的空间推理过程，设计基于情景语义缓冲队列的自适应双模态反思机制，主动验证目标可见性并利用历史记忆纠正决策以防止死锁。对于低层动作执行，使用深度掩码提取可行动作序列，让VLM选择最优动作映射到实际空间移动。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D数据集上的广泛评估表明，ReMemNav在成功率和探索效率方面优于现有的免训练零样本基线方法。具体而言，在HM3D v0.1上SR和SPL分别提升1.7%和7.0%，在HM3D v0.2上分别提升18.2%和11.1%，在MP3D上分别提升8.7%和7.9%。

Conclusion: ReMemNav通过整合全景语义先验和情景记忆，有效解决了VLM在零样本物体导航中的空间幻觉、探索死锁和语义-控制脱节问题，显著提升了导航性能，为免训练导航系统提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot object navigation requires agents to locate unseen target objects in unfamiliar environments without prior maps or task-specific training which remains a significant challenge. Although recent advancements in vision-language models(VLMs) provide promising commonsense reasoning capabilities for this task, these models still suffer from spatial hallucinations, local exploration deadlocks, and a disconnect between high-level semantic intent and low-level control. In this regard, we propose a novel hierarchical navigation framework named ReMemNav, which seamlessly integrates panoramic semantic priors and episodic memory with VLMs. We introduce the Recognize Anything Model to anchor the spatial reasoning process of the VLM. We also design an adaptive dual-modal rethinking mechanism based on an episodic semantic buffer queue. The proposed mechanism actively verifies target visibility and corrects decisions using historical memory to prevent deadlocks. For low-level action execution, ReMemNav extracts a sequence of feasible actions using depth masks, allowing the VLM to select the optimal action for mapping into actual spatial movement. Extensive evaluations on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate that ReMemNav outperforms existing training-free zero-shot baselines in both success rate and exploration efficiency. Specifically, we achieve significant absolute performance improvements, with SR and SPL increasing by 1.7% and 7.0% on HM3D v0.1, 18.2% and 11.1% on HM3D v0.2, and 8.7% and 7.9% on MP3D.

</details>


### [374] [SpatialAnt: Autonomous Zero-Shot Robot Navigation via Active Scene Reconstruction and Visual Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26837)
*Jiwen Zhang,Xiangyu Shi,Siyuan Wang,Zerui Li,Zhongyu Wei,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpatialAnt：针对不完美自建场景重建的零样本视觉语言导航框架，通过物理接地和视觉预期机制提升在嘈杂环境中的导航性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于探索的零样本VLN方法依赖高质量人工场景重建，不适用于机器人实际部署。机器人自建的重建通常不完整且嘈杂，严重影响依赖高质量重建的方法性能

Method: 1) 物理接地策略：恢复单目重建的绝对度量尺度；2) 视觉预期机制：利用嘈杂点云渲染未来观测，进行反事实推理，修剪与人类指令矛盾的路径

Result: 在R2R-CE上达到66%成功率，RxR-CE上达到50.8%成功率。在Hello Robot上实际部署实现52%成功率，显著优于现有零样本方法

Conclusion: SpatialAnt通过处理不完美自建重建的挑战，有效弥合了嘈杂场景重建与鲁棒导航执行之间的差距，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出色

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has recently benefited from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), enabling zero-shot navigation. While recent exploration-based zero-shot methods have shown promising results by leveraging global scene priors, they rely on high-quality human-crafted scene reconstructions, which are impractical for real-world robot deployment. When encountering an unseen environment, a robot should build its own priors through pre-exploration. However, these self-built reconstructions are inevitably incomplete and noisy, which severely degrade methods that depend on high-quality scene reconstructions. To address these issues, we propose SpatialAnt, a zero-shot navigation framework designed to bridge the gap between imperfect self-reconstructions and robust execution. SpatialAnt introduces a physical grounding strategy to recover the absolute metric scale for monocular-based reconstructions. Furthermore, rather than treating the noisy self-reconstructed scenes as absolute spatial references, we propose a novel visual anticipation mechanism. This mechanism leverages the noisy point clouds to render future observations, enabling the agent to perform counterfactual reasoning and prune paths that contradict human instructions. Extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that SpatialAnt significantly outperforms existing zero-shot methods. We achieve a 66% Success Rate (SR) on R2R-CE and 50.8% SR on RxR-CE benchmarks. Physical deployment on a Hello Robot further confirms the efficiency and efficacy of our framework, achieving a 52% SR in challenging real-world settings.

</details>


### [375] [SCRAMPPI: Efficient Contingency Planning for Mobile Robot Navigation via Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26995)
*Raj Harshit Srirangam,Leonard Jung,Rohith Poola,Michael Everett*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合哈密顿-雅可比可达性分析与采样规划的方法，为自主机器人提供名义轨迹和应急计划，确保硬安全约束的同时提高采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人通常专注于最小化成本完成名义行为，但在故障或意外场景下容易失败。需要应急计划确保安全，但现有方法要么放松硬约束，要么使用昂贵的采样策略。

Method: 将应急可行性要求形式化为可达-避免问题，利用哈密顿-雅可比可达性分析进行认证。在线计算安全集的后向可达集值函数，通过重采样rollouts与MPPI采样规划器集成。

Result: 在对抗性规避任务的移动机器人上进行了仿真和硬件实验，算法能够实时生成名义轨迹和应急计划，保证硬约束满足的同时大幅提高采样效率。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了自主机器人应急规划的硬约束问题，通过可达性分析与采样规划的结合，实现了实时、安全的轨迹优化。

Abstract: Autonomous robots commonly aim to complete a nominal behavior while minimizing a cost; this leaves them vulnerable to failure or unplanned scenarios, where a backup or contingency plan to a safe set is needed to avoid a total mission failure. This is formalized as a trajectory optimization problem over the nominal cost with a safety constraint: from any point along the nominal plan, a feasible trajectory to a designated safe set must exist. Previous methods either relax this hard constraint, or use an expensive sampling-based strategy to optimize for this constraint. Instead, we formalize this requirement as a reach-avoid problem and leverage Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis to certify contingency feasibility. By computing the value function of our safe-set's backward reachable set online as the environment is revealed and integrating it with a sampling based planner (MPPI) via resampling based rollouts, we guarantee satisfaction of the hard constraint while greatly increasing sampling efficiency. Finally, we present simulated and hardware experiments demonstrating our algorithm generating nominal and contingency plans in real time on a mobile robot in an adversarial evasion task.

</details>


### [376] [ROSClaw: An OpenClaw ROS 2 Framework for Agentic Robot Control and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26997)
*Irvin Steve Cardenas,Marcus Anthony Arnett,Natalie Catherine Yeo,Lucky Sah,Jong-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSClaw是一个模型无关的执行层，将OpenClaw代理运行时与ROS 2集成，使任何基础模型都能通过标准化接口感知、推理和控制任何ROS机器人，无需定制集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将基础模型连接到物理机器人需要针对特定模型和平台的定制集成，这限制了模型的通用性和可移植性。需要一种标准化的方法来连接不同基础模型与各种机器人平台。

Method: 开发ROSClaw执行层，包含四个核心功能：(1) 动态能力发现与标准化功能注入；(2) 多模态观察归一化；(3) 预执行动作验证与可配置安全边界；(4) 结构化审计日志记录。支持模型后端和机器人平台的灵活配置。

Result: 在三个平台（轮式、四足、人形）和四个基础模型后端上成功部署。发现不同模型在策略外动作提议率上存在显著差异（高达4.8倍），相同命令产生不同的物理行为。与ROSA的对比验证表明执行层设计显著影响任务完成和安全行为。

Conclusion: ROSClaw既是实用的机器人代理基础设施，也是可重复的具身AI测量工具。它解决了基础模型与机器人集成的标准化问题，使模型交换和平台更换仅需配置更改，同时保持工具模式、安全执行和溯源日志的一致性。
