Abstract: Foundation models can endow robots with open-ended reasoning, language understanding, and adaptive planning, yet connecting a model to a physical robot today requires bespoke integration that couples perception, actuation, and safety to a single model and platform. We present ROSClaw, a model-agnostic executive layer that integrates the OpenClaw agent runtime with ROS 2, enabling any foundation model to perceive, reason about, and act on any ROS-enabled robot through (i) dynamic capability discovery with standardized affordance injection, (ii) multimodal observation normalization, (iii) pre-execution action validation within a configurable safety envelope, and (iv) structured audit logging. Swapping model backends or robot platforms is a configuration change; tool schemas, safety enforcement, and provenance logging remain invariant. We deploy ROSClaw on three platforms (wheeled, quadruped, humanoid) with four foundation-model backends. Under this controlled substrate, models exhibit up to 4.8 x differences in out-of-policy action proposal rates (3.4 x among frontier models alone) and produce qualitatively distinct physical behaviors from identical commands. A cross-framework parity protocol against ROSA confirms that executive-layer design, not just prompt wording, significantly affects both task completion and safety behavior, establishing ROSClaw as both practical agentic-robot infrastructure and a reproducible measurement instrument for embodied AI.

</details>


### [377] [UMI-Underwater: Learning Underwater Manipulation without Underwater Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27012)
*Hao Li,Long Yin Chung,Jack Goler,Ryan Zhang,Xiaochi Xie,Huy Ha,Shuran Song,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个水下机器人抓取系统，通过自监督数据收集和基于深度的affordance表示实现从陆地到水下领域的零样本知识迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人抓取面临挑战：图像质量差、变化大，且收集多样化水下演示数据成本高昂。需要解决从陆地到水下领域的知识迁移问题

Method: 1) 自监督数据收集管道自动收集成功的水下抓取演示；2) 使用基于深度的affordance表示桥接陆地到水下领域差距，对光照和颜色变化具有鲁棒性；3) 在陆地手持演示上训练的affordance模型通过几何对齐零样本部署到水下；4) 基于affordance的扩散策略在水下演示上训练生成控制动作

Result: 在泳池实验中，该方法提高了抓取性能和背景变化的鲁棒性，能够泛化到仅在陆地数据中见过的物体，优于仅使用RGB的基线方法

Conclusion: 提出的系统通过自监督数据收集和深度affordance表示，成功实现了从陆地到水下领域的抓取知识迁移，解决了水下机器人抓取的数据收集和领域适应问题

Abstract: Underwater robotic grasping is difficult due to degraded, highly variable imagery and the expense of collecting diverse underwater demonstrations. We introduce a system that (i) autonomously collects successful underwater grasp demonstrations via a self-supervised data collection pipeline and (ii) transfers grasp knowledge from on-land human demonstrations through a depth-based affordance representation that bridges the on-land-to-underwater domain gap and is robust to lighting and color shift. An affordance model trained on on-land handheld demonstrations is deployed underwater zero-shot via geometric alignment, and an affordance-conditioned diffusion policy is then trained on underwater demonstrations to generate control actions. In pool experiments, our approach improves grasping performance and robustness to background shifts, and enables generalization to objects seen only in on-land data, outperforming RGB-only baselines. Code, videos, and additional results are available at https://umi-under-water.github.io.

</details>


### [378] [An End-to-end Flight Control Network for High-speed UAV Obstacle Avoidance based on Event-Depth Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27181)
*Dikai Shang,Jingyue Zhao,Shi Xu,Nanyang Ye,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双向交叉注意力模块的端到端飞行控制网络，通过特征级融合深度图像和事件数据，实现复杂环境中高速自主飞行的可靠避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一感知模态在复杂环境中存在局限性：深度相机对静态物体有效但在高速下易产生运动模糊，事件相机擅长捕捉快速运动但难以感知静态场景。需要融合两种传感器的互补优势来实现高速自主飞行。

Method: 1. 设计端到端飞行控制网络，通过双向交叉注意力模块实现深度图像和事件数据的特征级融合；2. 采用模仿学习训练网络，依赖高质量监督；3. 设计基于球形主搜索(SPS)的高效专家规划器，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降低到O(n)并生成更平滑轨迹。

Result: 1. SPS规划器在17m/s速度下达到超过80%的成功率，比传统规划器提高近20%；2. 仿真实验中，该方法在17m/s速度下在不同场景中达到70-80%的成功率，比单模态和单向融合模型提高10-20%；3. 双向融合能有效整合事件和深度信息，在静态和动态物体混合的复杂环境中实现更可靠的避障。

Conclusion: 双向特征级融合深度图像和事件数据的方法能够有效结合两种传感器的互补优势，显著提高复杂环境中高速自主飞行的避障成功率，为动态和静态障碍物混合环境下的可靠飞行控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving safe, high-speed autonomous flight in complex environments with static, dynamic, or mixed obstacles remains challenging, as a single perception modality is incomplete. Depth cameras are effective for static objects but suffer from motion blur at high speeds. Conversely, event cameras excel at capturing rapid motion but struggle to perceive static scenes. To exploit the complementary strengths of both sensors, we propose an end-to-end flight control network that achieves feature-level fusion of depth images and event data through a bidirectional crossattention module. The end-to-end network is trained via imitation learning, which relies on high-quality supervision. Building on this insight, we design an efficient expert planner using Spherical Principal Search (SPS). This planner reduces computational complexity from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$ while generating smoother trajectories, achieving over 80% success rate at 17m/s--nearly 20% higher than traditional planners. Simulation experiments show that our method attains a 70-80% success rate at 17 m/s across varied scenes, surpassing single-modality and unidirectional fusion models by 10-20%. These results demonstrate that bidirectional fusion effectively integrates event and depth information, enabling more reliable obstacle avoidance in complex environments with both static and dynamic objects.

</details>


### [379] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc Networks-Based Multi-Target Tracking Based on Scene-Adaptive Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27194)
*Kai Tian,Jialun Wang,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Ying Liu,Qian Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向多AUV自组织网络的场景自适应具身智能架构，通过三层功能框架和双路径批评家机制的SA-MARL算法，显著提升多目标跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统以数据为中心的架构在高度动态拓扑变化和严重受限的水声通信带宽下难以维持操作一致性，需要新的解决方案来应对复杂海上任务。

Method: 提出场景自适应具身智能架构，将AUV重新构想为具身实体，集成感知、决策和物理执行到统一认知循环。采用三层功能框架和SA-MARL算法，该算法包含场景批评家网络和通用批评家网络的双路径批评家机制，通过权重动态融合实现专门跟踪任务与全局安全约束的解耦。

Result: 评估结果表明，该方案显著加速策略收敛，相比主流MARL方法获得更优的跟踪精度，即使在强烈环境干扰和流体拓扑变化下仍保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 提出的场景自适应具身智能架构有效解决了水下自组织网络在多目标跟踪任务中的动态拓扑和通信约束问题，为复杂海上任务执行提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of underwater net-working and multi-agent coordination technologies, autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) ad-hoc networks have emerged as a pivotal framework for executing complex maritime missions, such as multi-target tracking. However, traditional data-centricarchitectures struggle to maintain operational consistency under highly dynamic topological fluctuations and severely constrained acoustic communication bandwidth. This article proposes a scene-adaptive embodied intelligence (EI) architecture for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks, which re-envisions AUVs as embodied entities by integrating perception, decision-making, and physical execution into a unified cognitive loop. To materialize the functional interaction between these layers, we define a beacon-based communication and control model that treats the communication link as a dynamic constraint-aware channel, effectively bridging the gap between high-level policy inference and decentralized physical actuation. Specifically, the proposed architecture employs a three-layer functional framework and introduces a Scene-Adaptive MARL (SA-MARL) algorithm featuring a dual-path critic mechanism. By integrating a scene critic network and a general critic network through a weight-based dynamic fusion process, SA-MARL effectively decouples specialized tracking tasks from global safety constraints, facilitating autonomous policy evolution. Evaluation results demonstrate that the proposedscheme significantly accelerates policy convergence and achieves superior tracking accuracy compared to mainstream MARL approaches, maintaining robust performance even under intense environmental interference and fluid topological shifts.

</details>


### [380] [Autonomous overtaking trajectory optimization using reinforcement learning and opponent pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27207)
*Matej Rene Cihlar,Luka Šiktar,Branimir Ćaran,Marko Švaco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于强化学习的多智能体自动驾驶赛车超车轨迹优化系统，融合LiDAR和深度相机数据，使用UKF进行目标姿态估计，在仿真和真实实验中成功实现超车


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆超车是自动驾驶中最复杂的驾驶操作之一，需要多传感器支持以实现安全的轨迹优化和超车效率。现有系统需要更有效的多智能体赛车环境中的超车解决方案。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的多智能体自主赛车超车机制，融合LiDAR（2D检测算法）和深度相机（YOLO目标检测）数据，使用无迹卡尔曼滤波器（UKF）进行对手姿态估计和轨迹优化，利用预生成的赛道线和传感器输入计算转向角和线速度。

Result: 算法在仿真和真实世界实验中成功执行超车操作，姿态估计RMSE达到(0.0816, 0.0531)米（x,y方向），证明了系统的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习机制结合多传感器融合技术，能够有效优化超车轨迹，在多智能体赛车环境中实现安全高效的超车操作，为自动驾驶超车问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicle overtaking is one of the most complex driving maneuvers for autonomous vehicles. To achieve optimal autonomous overtaking, driving systems rely on multiple sensors that enable safe trajectory optimization and overtaking efficiency. This paper presents a reinforcement learning mechanism for multi-agent autonomous racing environments, enabling overtaking trajectory optimization, based on LiDAR and depth image data. The developed reinforcement learning agent uses pre-generated raceline data and sensor inputs to compute the steering angle and linear velocity for optimal overtaking. The system uses LiDAR with a 2D detection algorithm and a depth camera with YOLO-based object detection to identify the vehicle to be overtaken and its pose. The LiDAR and the depth camera detection data are fused using a UKF for improved opponent pose estimation and trajectory optimization for overtaking in racing scenarios. The results show that the proposed algorithm successfully performs overtaking maneuvers in both simulation and real-world experiments, with pose estimation RMSE of (0.0816, 0.0531) m in (x, y).

</details>


### [381] [Design of an In-Pipe Robot with Contact-Angle-Guided Kinematic Decoupling for Crosstalk-Suppressed Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27245)
*Min Yang,Yang Tian,Longchuang Li,Jun Ma,Shugen Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种V形管道检测机器人，采用关节轴与轮分离布局，仅用两个电机实现全轮驱动推进和电动滚动重定向，通过几何传输分析抑制交叉干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统V形管道机器人中，轴向牵引和滚动重定向功能通常依赖共享接触或间接驱动，导致强烈的运动耦合，性能对几何和摩擦变化敏感。

Method: 设计关节轴与轮分离布局，提供两个物理独立的驱动通道；建立驱动传输矩阵，识别球形轮接触角作为关键几何变量；进行几何传输分析和静态稳定性建模。

Result: 实验验证了解耦效果：在垂直管道中进行高动态滚动时，推进扭矩几乎保持不变；在包含平面外双弯头的多材料测试平台上，机器人实现了100%的成功率。

Conclusion: 提出的V形管道机器人通过几何设计成功实现驱动解耦，提高了在复杂管道网络中的可靠性和稳定性，为紧凑型管道机器人设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In-pipe inspection robots must traverse confined pipeline networks with elbows and three-dimensional fittings, requiring both reliable axial traction and rapid rolling reorientation for posture correction. In compact V-shaped platforms, these functions often rely on shared contacts or indirect actuation, which introduces strong kinematic coupling and makes performance sensitive to geometry and friction variations. This paper presents a V-shaped in-pipe robot with a joint-axis-and-wheel-separation layout that provides two physically independent actuation channels, with all-wheel-drive propulsion and motorized rolling reorientation while using only two motors. To make the decoupling mechanism explicit and designable, we formulate an actuation transmission matrix and identify the spherical-wheel contact angle as the key geometric variable governing the dominant roll-to-propulsion leakage and roll-channel efficiency. A geometric transmission analysis maps mounting parameters to the contact angle, leakage, and efficiency, yielding a structural guideline for suppressing crosstalk by driving the contact angle toward zero. A static stability model further provides a stability-domain map for selecting torsion-spring stiffness under friction uncertainty to ensure vertical-pipe stability with a margin. Experiments validate the decoupling effect, where during high-dynamic rolling in a vertical pipe, the propulsion torque remains nearly invariant. On a multi-material testbed including out-of-plane double elbows, the robot achieved a 100% success rate in more than 10 independent round-trip trials.

</details>


### [382] [Robust Global-Local Behavior Arbitration via Continuous Command Fusion Under LiDAR Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27273)
*Mohamed Elgouhary,Amr S. El-Wakeel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种ROS2原生仲裁模块，融合全局Pure Pursuit控制器和局部Gap Follow控制器的输出，通过PPO训练的策略进行连续门控，在感知受损条件下评估自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化自动驾驶系统需要在全局进度目标和局部安全反应之间协调，同时面临不完美感知和严格实时约束的挑战。需要一种方法在保持控制器可解释性的同时，提高系统在感知受损情况下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ROS2原生仲裁模块，融合两个未修改的可解释控制器：基于Pure Pursuit的全局参考跟踪控制器和基于LiDAR的Gap Follow反应式控制器。使用PPO训练的策略从紧凑特征观察中预测连续门控值，结合两个控制器的Ackermann命令输出，并加入实际安全检查。同时实现轻量级基于采样的预测基线作为对比。

Result: 在可重复的近距离通过场景中，使用ROS2损伤协议注入LiDAR噪声、延迟和丢包，并扫描前向锥形假短程异常值。报告了安全成功率、失败率以及每步端到端控制器运行时间随感知压力增加的变化。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在作为模块化ROS2设置中的命令级鲁棒性评估，而不是替代规划级的交互推理。提出的仲裁方法能够在感知受损条件下保持系统安全性，同时保持控制器的可解释性。

Abstract: Modular autonomous driving systems must coordinate global progress objectives with local safety-driven reactions under imperfect sensing and strict real-time constraints. This paper presents a ROS2-native arbitration module that continuously fuses the outputs of two unchanged and interpretable controllers: a global reference-tracking controller based on Pure Pursuit and a reactive LiDAR-based Gap Follow controller. At each control step, both controllers propose Ackermann commands, and a PPO-trained policy predicts a continuous gate from a compact feature observation to produce a single fused drive command, augmented with practical safety checks. For comparison under identical ROS topic inputs and control rate, we implement a lightweight sampling-based predictive baseline. Robustness is evaluated using a ROS2 impairment protocol that injects LiDAR noise, delay, and dropout, and additionally sweeps forward-cone false short-range outliers. In a repeatable close-proximity passing scenario, we report safe success and failure rates together with per-step end-to-end controller runtime as sensing stress increases. The study is intended as a command-level robustness evaluation in a modular ROS2 setting, not as a replacement for planning-level interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [383] [HiFlow: Tokenization-Free Scale-Wise Autoregressive Policy Learning via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27281)
*Daichi Yashima,Koki Seno,Shuhei Kurita,Yusuke Oda,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiFlow：一种无需标记化的粗到细自回归策略，直接在原始连续动作上操作，通过简单的时间池化构建多尺度连续动作目标，实现端到端单阶段训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到细自回归方法依赖离散动作标记器，将连续动作序列映射为码本索引，这种设计继承自图像生成领域。然而机器人动作本质上是低维连续向量，这种标记化会引入不必要的量化误差和复杂的多阶段训练流程。

Method: HiFlow采用无标记化的粗到细自回归策略，直接操作原始连续动作。通过简单的时间池化构建多尺度连续动作目标：对连续动作窗口进行平均以产生粗粒度摘要，然后在更细的时间分辨率上进行细化。整个模型端到端单阶段训练，无需单独标记器。

Result: 在MimicGen、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界环境中的实验表明，HiFlow持续优于现有方法，包括基于扩散的策略和基于标记化的自回归策略。

Conclusion: HiFlow证明了对于低维连续机器人动作，直接操作原始连续动作的无标记化粗到细自回归方法比依赖离散标记化的方法更有效，简化了训练流程并提高了性能。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine autoregressive modeling has recently shown strong promise for visuomotor policy learning, combining the inference efficiency of autoregressive methods with the global trajectory coherence of diffusion-based policies. However, existing approaches rely on discrete action tokenizers that map continuous action sequences to codebook indices, a design inherited from image generation where learned compression is necessary for high-dimensional pixel data. We observe that robot actions are inherently low-dimensional continuous vectors, for which such tokenization introduces unnecessary quantization error and a multi-stage training pipeline. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Flow Policy (HiFlow), a tokenization-free coarse-to-fine autoregressive policy that operates directly on raw continuous actions. HiFlow constructs multi-scale continuous action targets from each action chunk via simple temporal pooling. Specifically, it averages contiguous action windows to produce coarse summaries that are refined at finer temporal resolutions. The entire model is trained end-to-end in a single stage, eliminating the need for a separate tokenizer. Experiments on MimicGen, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world environments demonstrate that HiFlow consistently outperforms existing methods including diffusion-based and tokenization-based autoregressive policies.

</details>


### [384] [Uni-World VLA: Interleaved World Modeling and Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27287)
*Qiqi Liu,Huan Xu,Jingyu Li,Bin Sun,Zhihui Hao,Dangen She,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-World VLA：一种统一视觉-语言-动作模型，通过交替预测未来帧和轨迹规划，实现世界建模与控制的闭环交互，提升自动驾驶决策适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的方法通常先预测未来场景再规划，导致开环想象可能偏离实际决策过程。需要更紧密地结合世界建模与规划，实现更自适应的动态交通场景决策。

Method: 提出统一视觉-语言-动作模型，交替进行未来帧预测和自我动作规划，形成闭环交互。同时将单目深度信息融入帧中，为世界建模提供更强的几何线索。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准测试中，该方法实现了具有竞争力的闭环规划性能，同时生成高保真度的未来帧预测。

Conclusion: 紧密耦合世界预测与规划是构建可扩展VLA驾驶系统的有前景方向，闭环交互能提升动态场景中的决策适应性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires reasoning about how the environment evolves and planning actions accordingly. Existing world-model-based approaches typically predict future scenes first and plan afterwards, resulting in open-loop imagination that may drift from the actual decision process. In this paper, we present Uni-World VLA, a unified vision-language-action (VLA) model that tightly interleaves future frame prediction and trajectory planning. Instead of generating a full world rollout before planning, our model alternates between predicting future frames and ego actions step by step, allowing planning decisions to be continuously conditioned on the imagined future observations. This interleaved generation forms a closed-loop interaction between world modeling and control, enabling more adaptive decision-making in dynamic traffic scenarios. In addition, we incorporate monocular depth information into frames to provide stronger geometric cues for world modeling, improving long-horizon scene prediction. Experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that our approach achieves competitive closed-loop planning performance while producing high-fidelity future frame predictions. These results demonstrate that tightly coupling world prediction and planning is a promising direction for scalable VLA driving systems.

</details>


### [385] [MetaTune: Adjoint-based Meta-tuning via Robotic Differentiable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27313)
*Xiexin Peng,Bingheng Wang,Tao Zhang,Ying Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaTune：基于可微分闭环元学习的统一框架，用于联合自动调整反馈控制器和扰动观测器，通过伴随方法高效计算元梯度，在四旋翼控制中显著提升性能并减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扰动观测器的控制方法在增强机器人系统鲁棒性方面表现出潜力，但由于控制器增益和观测器参数之间的强耦合，这类系统的调参仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出MetaTune框架，通过可微分闭环元学习联合自动调整反馈控制器和扰动观测器。框架整合了便携式神经策略和从可微分系统动力学导出的物理信息梯度，实现跨任务和操作条件的自适应增益。开发伴随方法，在时间上反向高效计算自适应增益的元梯度，直接最小化代价函数。

Result: 在四旋翼控制实验中，MetaTune相比现有可微分调参方法取得一致改进，同时梯度计算时间减少50%以上。在高保真PX4-Gazebo硬件在环仿真中，学习到的自适应策略在激进飞行速度下平均跟踪误差减少15-20%，在强扰动下提升高达40%，且无需微调即可实现零样本仿真到仿真迁移。

Conclusion: MetaTune为控制器和扰动观测器的联合自动调参提供了高效统一的框架，通过伴随方法显著降低计算复杂度，在四旋翼控制中展现出优越的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Disturbance observer-based control has shown promise in robustifying robotic systems against uncertainties. However, tuning such systems remains challenging due to the strong coupling between controller gains and observer parameters. In this work, we propose MetaTune, a unified framework for joint auto-tuning of feedback controllers and disturbance observers through differentiable closed-loop meta-learning. MetaTune integrates a portable neural policy with physics-informed gradients derived from differentiable system dynamics, enabling adaptive gain across tasks and operating conditions. We develop an adjoint method that efficiently computes the meta-gradients with respect to adaptive gains backward in time to directly minimize the cost-to-go. Compared to existing forward methods, our approach reduces the computational complexity to be linear in the data horizon. Experimental results on quadrotor control show that MetaTune achieves consistent improvements over state-of-the-art differentiable tuning methods while reducing gradient computation time by more than 50 percent. In high-fidelity PX4-Gazebo hardware-in-the-loop simulation, the learned adaptive policy yields 15-20 percent average tracking error reduction at aggressive flight speeds and up to 40 percent improvement under strong disturbances, while demonstrating zero-shot sim-to-sim transfer without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [386] [Where-to-Learn: Analytical Policy Gradient Directed Exploration for On-Policy Robotic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27317)
*Leixin Chang,Xinchen Yao,Ben Liu,Liangjing Yang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可微动力学模型的分析策略梯度方法，通过任务感知、物理引导的定向探索来加速策略学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法通过最大化策略熵或鼓励访问新状态来探索，但这些方法不考虑状态潜在价值，导致探索效率低下。在机器人控制中，高效探索对高质量策略学习至关重要。

Method: 提出一种定向探索方法，使用可微动力学模型的分析策略梯度，注入任务感知和物理引导的指导，引导智能体向高奖励区域探索。

Result: 该方法能够更有效地引导智能体探索，加速策略学习过程，提高学习效率。

Conclusion: 通过任务感知、物理引导的定向探索，可以显著提高强化学习在机器人控制中的探索效率和策略学习质量。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have demonstrated great potential in robotic control, where effective exploration is crucial for efficient and high-quality policy learning. However, how to encourage the agent to explore the better trajectories efficiently remains a challenge. Most existing methods incentivize exploration by maximizing the policy entropy or encouraging novel state visiting regardless of the potential state value. We propose a new form of directed exploration that uses analytical policy gradients from a differentiable dynamics model to inject task-aware, physics-guided guidance, thereby steering the agent towards high-reward regions for accelerated and more effective policy learning.

</details>


### [387] [D-SPEAR: Dual-Stream Prioritized Experience Adaptive Replay for Stable Reinforcement Learninging Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27346)
*Yu Zhang,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-SPEAR：一种用于机器人强化学习的双流优先经验回放框架，通过分离actor和critic的采样策略，提升训练稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统off-policy算法（如SAC、TD3）在机器人操作任务中常出现策略震荡和性能崩溃问题，主要原因是经验回放策略未能区分actor和critic的不同数据需求。

Method: 提出D-SPEAR框架：1）使用共享回放缓冲区但分离actor和critic采样；2）critic采用优先回放进行高效价值学习；3）actor使用低误差转移稳定策略优化；4）自适应锚机制平衡均匀和优先采样；5）Huber-based critic目标函数提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在robosuite基准测试的Block-Lifting和Door-Opening等机器人操作任务上，D-SPEAR在最终性能和训练稳定性方面均优于SAC、TD3、DDPG等基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦actor和critic的采样需求，D-SPEAR有效解决了机器人强化学习中的训练不稳定问题，消融研究证实了双流设计的互补作用。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation remains challenging for reinforcement learning due to contact-rich dynamics, long horizons, and training instability. Although off-policy actor-critic algorithms such as SAC and TD3 perform well in simulation, they often suffer from policy oscillations and performance collapse in realistic settings, partly due to experience replay strategies that ignore the differing data requirements of the actor and the critic. We propose D-SPEAR: Dual-Stream Prioritized Experience Adaptive Replay, a replay framework that decouples actor and critic sampling while maintaining a shared replay buffer. The critic leverages prioritized replay for efficient value learning, whereas the actor is updated using low-error transitions to stabilize policy optimization. An adaptive anchor mechanism balances uniform and prioritized sampling based on the coefficient of variation of TD errors, and a Huber-based critic objective further improves robustness under heterogeneous reward scales. We evaluate D-SPEAR on challenging robotic manipulation tasks from the robosuite benchmark, including Block-Lifting and Door-Opening. Results demonstrate that D-SPEAR consistently outperforms strong off-policy baselines, including SAC, TD3, and DDPG, in both final performance and training stability, with ablation studies confirming the complementary roles of the actorside and critic-side replay streams.

</details>


### [388] [Online Inertia Tensor Identification for Non-Cooperative Spacecraft via Augmented UKF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27361)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种增强型UKF框架，联合估计非合作目标航天器的6自由度相对位姿和完整惯性张量，融合单目视觉CNN和LiDAR深度信息，实现实时质量分布估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作或翻滚目标航天器的质量特性通常未知或不确定，导致基于模型的传播器快速发散，影响自主接近操作（如主动碎片清除、在轨服务）的高保真相对导航。

Method: 提出增强型UKF框架，将状态向量扩展至包含惯性张量的六个独立元素，融合单目视觉CNN的视觉测量和LiDAR的深度信息，采用自适应过程噪声公式确保数值稳定性和物理一致性。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证，增强型UKF能够同时实现运动学状态和惯性参数的收敛，支持准确的长时期轨迹预测和非合作深空环境中的鲁棒制导。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无需地面预校准的情况下实时恢复目标归一化质量分布，为存在参数不确定性的非合作航天器操作提供鲁棒的相对导航解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous proximity operations, such as active debris removal and on-orbit servicing, require high-fidelity relative navigation solutions that remain robust in the presence of parametric uncertainty. Standard estimation frameworks typically assume that the target spacecraft's mass properties are known a priori; however, for non-cooperative or tumbling targets, these parameters are often unknown or uncertain, leading to rapid divergence in model-based propagators. This paper presents an augmented Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) framework designed to jointly estimate the relative 6-DOF pose and the full inertia tensor of a non-cooperative target spacecraft. The proposed architecture fuses visual measurements from monocular vision-based Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) with depth information from LiDAR to constrain the coupled rigid-body dynamics. By augmenting the state vector to include the six independent elements of the inertia tensor, the filter dynamically recovers the target's normalized mass distribution in real-time without requiring ground-based pre-calibration. To ensure numerical stability and physical consistency during the estimation of constant parameters, the filter employs an adaptive process noise formulation that prevents covariance collapse while allowing for the gradual convergence of the inertial parameters. Numerical validation is performed via Monte Carlo simulations, demonstrating that the proposed Augmented UKF enables the simultaneous convergence of kinematic states and inertial parameters, thereby facilitating accurate long-term trajectory prediction and robust guidance in non-cooperative deep-space environments.

</details>


### [389] [Rainbow-DemoRL: Combining Improvements in Demonstration-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27400)
*Dwait Bhatt,Shih-Chieh Chou,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究对利用离线演示数据提升在线强化学习样本效率的三种方法进行了系统比较，发现直接重用离线数据和行为克隆初始化比复杂的离线RL预训练方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有多种利用离线演示数据提升在线强化学习样本效率的方法，但哪种方法最有效、能否组合使用、是否有累积效益尚不清楚，需要系统比较。

Method: 将现有演示增强RL方法分为三类，进行广泛的实证研究，分析每种策略的优缺点和组合效果，以确定有效的混合策略。

Result: 研究发现直接重用离线数据和行为克隆初始化在提升在线样本效率方面，始终优于更复杂的离线RL预训练方法。

Conclusion: 对于提升在线强化学习的样本效率，简单直接的方法（重用离线数据和行为克隆）比复杂的离线RL预训练更有效，这为实际应用提供了指导。

Abstract: Several approaches have been proposed to improve the sample efficiency of online reinforcement learning (RL) by leveraging demonstrations collected offline. The offline data can be used directly as transitions to optimize RL objectives, or offline policy and value functions can first be learned from the data and then used for online finetuning or to provide reference actions. While each of these strategies has shown compelling results, it is unclear which method has the most impact on sample efficiency, whether these approaches can be combined, and if there are cumulative benefits. We classify existing demonstration-augmented RL approaches into three categories and perform an extensive empirical study of their strengths, weaknesses, and combinations to isolate the contribution of each strategy and determine effective hybrid combinations for sample-efficient online RL. Our analysis reveals that directly reusing offline data and initializing with behavior cloning consistently outperform more complex offline RL pretraining methods for improving online sample efficiency.

</details>


### [390] [Agent-Driven Autonomous Reinforcement Learning Research: Iterative Policy Improvement for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27416)
*Nimesh Khandelwal,Shakti S. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文记录了一个四足机器人运动控制中智能体驱动自主强化学习研究的案例研究，展示了智能体在人类高层指导下能够执行大部分RL研究循环，从早期粗糙地形到最终实现97%超时率的稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在机器人强化学习领域中，智能体能否在有限人类干预下自主执行迭代式研究循环，特别是在多GPU实验管理和模拟器特定工程约束的复杂环境中。

Method: 采用智能体驱动的自主强化学习方法，人类通过智能编码环境提供高层指导，智能体负责执行代码阅读、故障诊断、奖励和地形配置编辑、作业启动监控、中间指标分析以及提出下一轮实验等任务。

Result: 在Isaac Lab的DHAV1 12-DoF四足机器人上进行了14轮70多个实验，从早期平均奖励约7的粗糙地形运行，发展到Wave 12的最佳记录运行exp063，速度误差0.263，在2000次迭代中达到97%超时率，并在不同GPU上独立复现5次。

Conclusion: 研究表明智能体能够在该领域实质性执行迭代式RL研究循环，同时明确了人类指导仍然在议程制定中发挥重要作用，为自主机器人研究提供了实证和文档化贡献。

Abstract: This paper documents a case study in agent-driven autonomous reinforcement learning research for quadruped locomotion. The setting was not a fully self-starting research system. A human provided high-level directives through an agentic coding environment, while an agent carried out most of the execution loop: reading code, diagnosing failures, editing reward and terrain configurations, launching and monitoring jobs, analyzing intermediate metrics, and proposing the next wave of experiments. Across more than 70 experiments organized into fourteen waves on a DHAV1 12-DoF quadruped in Isaac Lab, the agent progressed from early rough-terrain runs with mean reward around 7 to a best logged Wave 12 run, exp063, with velocity error 0.263 and 97\% timeout over 2000 iterations, independently reproduced five times across different GPUs. The archive also records several concrete autonomous research decisions: isolating PhysX deadlocks to terrain sets containing boxes and stair-like primitives, porting four reward terms from openly available reference implementations \cite{deeprobotics, rlsar}, correcting Isaac Sim import and bootstrapping issues, reducing environment count for diagnosis, terminating hung runs, and pivoting effort away from HIM after repeated terrain=0.0 outcomes. Relative to the AutoResearch paradigm \cite{autoresearch}, this case study operates in a more failure-prone robotics RL setting with multi-GPU experiment management and simulator-specific engineering constraints. The contribution is empirical and documentary: it shows that an agent can materially execute the iterative RL research loop in this domain with limited human intervention, while also making clear where human direction still shaped the agenda.

</details>


### [391] [Predictive Modeling in AUV Navigation: A Perspective from Kalman Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27422)
*Zizhan Tang,Yao Liu,Jessica Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向AUV的安全框架，通过融合TDOA声学定位与卡尔曼滤波预测，提升定位精度和轨迹稳定性，并在通信中断时提供基于不确定性传播的搜索恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主水下航行器在通信中断时的定位精度不足和搜索恢复困难问题，需要提高实时跟踪的鲁棒性并为通信丢失后的搜索操作提供有效支持。

Method: 使用固定浮标网络检测AUV发出的声学信号，计算到达时间差(TDOA)测距差作为位置观测，然后将这些观测与基于卡尔曼滤波的预测模型融合，获得连续、抗噪声的状态估计。搜索模块区分持续导航和推进故障，预测通信中断后的运动并显式建模不确定性增长。

Result: 相比仅使用TDOA的基线方法，该框架实现了显著更好的定位精度和轨迹稳定性，能够提供鲁棒的搜索区域定义，支持通信中断期间的高效搜索恢复操作。

Conclusion: 该安全框架通过融合多浮标声学数据与卡尔曼滤波及不确定性传播，不仅提高了AUV的实时导航精度，还为通信中断情况下的搜索恢复提供了系统化解决方案，增强了AUV操作的安全性。

Abstract: We present a safety-oriented framework for autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) that improves localization accuracy, enhances trajectory prediction, and supports efficient search operations during communication loss. Acoustic signals emitted by the AUV are detected by a network of fixed buoys, which compute Time-Difference-of-Arrival (TDOA) range-difference measurements serving as position observations. These observations are subsequently fused with a Kalman-based prediction model to obtain continuous, noise-robust state estimates. The combined method achieves significantly better localization precision and trajectory stability than TDOA-only baselines.
  Beyond real-time tracking, our framework offers targeted search-and-recovery capability by predicting post-disconnection motion and explicitly modeling uncertainty growth. The search module differentiates between continued navigation and propulsion failure, allowing search resources to be deployed toward the most probable recovery region. Our framework fuses multi-buoy acoustic data with Kalman filtering and uncertainty propagation to maintain navigation accuracy and yield robust search-region definitions during communication loss.

</details>


### [392] [Robotic Dexterous Manipulation via Anisotropic Friction Modulation using Passive Rollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27452)
*Ethan Fisk,Taeyoon Lee,Shenli Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种配备可选择性制动或旋转的被动滚轮的机器人指尖设计，通过调制接触摩擦力和约束方向实现灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指尖摩擦控制对灵巧操作至关重要，但在机器人手中难以实现。需要一种低复杂度、机械高效的方法来调制摩擦，使机器人能够执行传统夹持器难以完成的灵巧动作。

Method: 设计配备被动滚轮的机器人指尖，滚轮可选择性地制动或旋转。未制动时，滚轮允许接触点沿滚动方向自由滑动；制动时，则像传统指尖一样抵抗运动。滚轮安装在旋转机构上，可重新定向约束框架以适应不同操作任务。开发了集成到平行夹持器中的基于约束的指尖模型。

Result: 实验表明，该设计能够实现传统机器人夹持器难以完成的各种灵巧动作，包括：抓握内的滑动和旋转、对不确定接触的鲁棒适应、多物体或多部件操作，以及需要指尖间不对称摩擦的交互。这些结果证明了被动滚轮指尖作为摩擦调制方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 被动滚轮指尖提供了一种低复杂度、机械高效的摩擦调制方法，推动了更适应性强、鲁棒的机器人操作技术的发展，能够支持多样化的操作策略。

Abstract: Controlling friction at the fingertip is fundamental to dexterous manipulation, yet remains difficult to realize in robotic hands. We present the design and analysis of a robotic fingertip equipped with passive rollers that can be selectively braked or pivoted to modulate contact friction and constraint directions. When unbraked, the rollers permit unconstrained sliding of the contact point along the rolling direction; when braked, they resist motion like a conventional fingertip. The rollers are mounted on a pivoting mechanism, allowing reorientation of the constraint frame to accommodate different manipulation tasks. We develop a constraint-based model of the fingertip integrated into a parallel-jaw gripper and analyze its ability to support diverse manipulation strategies. Experiments show that the proposed design enables a wide range of dexterous actions that are conventionally challenging for robotic grippers, including sliding and pivoting within the grasp, robust adaptation to uncertain contacts, multi-object or multi-part manipulation, and interactions requiring asymmetric friction across fingers. These results demonstrate the versatility of passive roller fingertips as a low-complexity, mechanically efficient approach to friction modulation, advancing the development of more adaptable and robust robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [393] [Copilot-Assisted Second-Thought Framework for Brain-to-Robot Hand Motion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27492)
*Yizhe Li,Shixiao Wang,Jian K. Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CNN-attention混合模型用于从EEG解码手部运动学，并扩展到EEG-EMG多模态解码，通过copilot框架提升轨迹保真度，最终用于控制机器人手臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN或RNN的运动学预测方法在处理长序列EEG数据时存在局限，而Transformer模型虽能建模长序列但计算成本高。需要开发更高效的方法来解码手部运动学，以构建运动相关脑机接口。

Method: 1) 提出CNN-attention混合模型解码EEG手部运动学；2) 扩展到EEG-EMG多模态解码；3) 引入copilot框架，使用运动状态感知critic在有限状态机中过滤低置信度解码点；4) 在MuJoCo仿真中控制Franka Panda机器人手臂。

Result: 单被试测试中，拇指和食指中点轨迹的X、Y、Z轴PCC值分别达到0.9854、0.9946、0.9065；跨被试测试结果为0.9643、0.9795、0.5852。copilot框架将EEG-only解码的总体PCC提升至0.93，且仅排除少于20%的数据点。

Conclusion: CNN-attention混合模型能有效解码手部运动学，EEG-EMG多模态解码显著提升性能，copilot框架可增强轨迹保真度，为运动相关脑机接口的实际应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Motor kinematics prediction (MKP) from electroencephalography (EEG) is an important research area for developing movement-related brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). While traditional methods often rely on convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or recurrent neural networks (RNNs), Transformer-based models have shown strong ability in modeling long sequential EEG data. In this study, we propose a CNN-attention hybrid model for decoding hand kinematics from EEG during grasp-and-lift tasks, achieving strong performance in within-subject experiments. We further extend this approach to EEG-EMG multimodal decoding, which yields substantially improved results. Within-subject tests achieve PCC values of 0.9854, 0.9946, and 0.9065 for the X, Y, and Z axes, respectively, computed on the midpoint trajectory between the thumb and index finger, while cross-subject tests result in 0.9643, 0.9795, and 0.5852. The decoded trajectories from both modalities are then used to control a Franka Panda robotic arm in a MuJoCo simulation. To enhance trajectory fidelity, we introduce a copilot framework that filters low-confidence decoded points using a motion-state-aware critic within a finite-state machine. This post-processing step improves the overall within-subject PCC of EEG-only decoding to 0.93 while excluding fewer than 20% of the data points.

</details>


### [394] [S3KF: Spherical State-Space Kalman Filtering for Panoramic 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27534)
*Zhongyuan Liu,Shaonan Yu,Jianping Li,Pengfei Wan,Xinhang Xu,Pengfei Wang,Maggie Y. Gao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S³KF：基于球面卡尔曼滤波的全景多目标跟踪框架，使用旋转LiDAR和鱼眼相机，在单位球面上实现几何一致的状态表示，实现分米级平面跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像平面跟踪器在全景图像中不可靠，传统欧几里得3D表示引入冗余方向参数，无法自然统一角度、尺度和深度估计。工业安全监控、广域机器人感知和大工作空间部署需要全景感知。

Method: 提出球面状态表示，在单位球面S²上使用切平面参数化建模目标方位，联合估计边界框尺度和深度动态。基于此状态推导扩展球面卡尔曼滤波管道，融合全景相机检测和LiDAR深度观测。建立基于地图的真值生成管道，使用可穿戴定位设备在共享全局LiDAR地图中注册。

Result: 在自采集真实世界序列上实现分米级平面跟踪精度，在动态场景中相比2D全景基线改进身份连续性，在Jetson AGX Orin平台上实现实时板载操作。

Conclusion: S³KF是全景感知和工业规模多目标跟踪的实用解决方案，提供几何一致的全景3D跟踪，克服传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Panoramic multi-object tracking is important for industrial safety monitoring, wide-area robotic perception, and infrastructure-light deployment in large workspaces. In these settings, the sensing system must provide full-surround coverage, metric geometric cues, and stable target association under wide field-of-view distortion and occlusion. Existing image-plane trackers are tightly coupled to the camera projection and become unreliable in panoramic imagery, while conventional Euclidean 3D formulations introduce redundant directional parameters and do not naturally unify angular, scale, and depth estimation. In this paper, we present $\mathbf{S^3KF}$, a panoramic 3D multi-object tracking framework built on a motorized rotating LiDAR and a quad-fisheye camera rig. The key idea is a geometry-consistent state representation on the unit sphere $\mathbb{S}^2$, where object bearing is modeled by a two-degree-of-freedom tangent-plane parameterization and jointly estimated with box scale and depth dynamics. Based on this state, we derive an extended spherical Kalman filtering pipeline that fuses panoramic camera detections with LiDAR depth observations for multimodal tracking. We further establish a map-based ground-truth generation pipeline using wearable localization devices registered to a shared global LiDAR map, enabling quantitative evaluation without motion-capture infrastructure. Experiments on self-collected real-world sequences show decimeter-level planar tracking accuracy, improved identity continuity over a 2D panoramic baseline in dynamic scenes, and real-time onboard operation on a Jetson AGX Orin platform. These results indicate that the proposed framework is a practical solution for panoramic perception and industrial-scale multi-object tracking.The project page can be found at https://kafeiyin00.github.io/S3KF/.

</details>


### [395] [Learning Smooth and Robust Space Robotic Manipulation of Dynamic Target via Inter-frame Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27537)
*Siyi Lang,Hongyi Gao,Yingxin Zhang,Zihao Liu,Hanlin Dong,Zhaoke Ning,Zhiqiang Ma,Panfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的空间机器人操控方法，通过整合历史时间信息和帧间相关机制，实现对未知非结构化环境中动态目标的精确操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务是未来航空航天工程的关键前沿，其中动态非合作目标的操控是关键技术。微重力环境下物体自由漂浮，缺乏地球上的支撑和摩擦约束，显著增加了空间机器人操控任务的复杂性。传统基于规划和控制的方主要局限于已知静态场景，缺乏实时响应能力。

Method: 提出数据驱动的空间机器人操控方法，整合历史时间信息和帧间相关机制。通过利用历史帧与当前帧之间的时间相关性，系统能有效捕捉场景中的运动特征，从而为动态目标生成稳定平滑的操控轨迹。

Result: 开发了基于地面的实验平台，包括PIPER X机械臂和双轴线性平台，精确模拟二维平面上的微重力自由漂浮运动，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够实现对未知非结构化空间环境中动态目标的精确机器人操控，解决了传统方法在实时响应和动态场景适应性方面的局限性。

Abstract: On-orbit servicing represents a critical frontier in future aerospace engineering, with the manipulation of dynamic non-cooperative targets serving as a key technology. In microgravity environments, objects are typically free-floating, lacking the support and frictional constraints found on Earth, which significantly escalates the complexity of tasks involving space robotic manipulation. Conventional planning and control-based methods are primarily limited to known, static scenarios and lack real-time responsiveness. To achieve precise robotic manipulation of dynamic targets in unknown and unstructured space environments, this letter proposes a data-driven space robotic manipulation approach that integrates historical temporal information and inter-frame correlation mechanisms. By exploiting the temporal correlation between historical and current frames, the system can effectively capture motion features within the scene, thereby producing stable and smooth manipulation trajectories for dynamic targets. To validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, we developed a ground-based experimental platform consisting of a PIPER X robotic arm and a dual-axis linear stage, which accurately simulates micro-gravity free-floating motion in a 2D plane.

</details>


### [396] [LLM-Enabled Low-Altitude UAV Natural Language Navigation via Signal Temporal Logic Specification Translation and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27583)
*Yuqi Ping,Huahao Ding,Tianhao Liang,Longyu Zhou,Guangyu Lei,Xinglin Chen,Junwei Wu,Jieyu Zhou,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，将自然语言指令转换为信号时序逻辑规范，并通过混合整数线性规划合成无人机轨迹，实现城市环境中安全、可解释的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言导航为低空无人机提供了智能便捷的解决方案，但在城市环境中需要将模糊指令精确转换为安全关键、动态可行的运动规划，同时满足时空约束。

Method: 开发了一个统一框架：1）使用基于思维链监督和组相对策略优化的推理增强大语言模型将自然语言指令转换为STL规范；2）引入规范修复机制，结合MILP诊断和LLM引导的语义推理来选择性放松任务约束。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实飞行实验表明，该闭环框架显著提高了自然语言到STL转换的鲁棒性，实现了复杂场景下安全、可解释和自适应的无人机导航。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过将自然语言指令转换为形式化STL规范并合成轨迹，解决了城市环境中无人机自然语言导航的挑战，确保了安全性和适应性。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) navigation for low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) offers an intelligent and convenient solution for low-altitude aerial services by enabling an intuitive interface for non-expert operators. However, deploying this capability in urban environments necessitates the precise grounding of underspecified instructions into safety-critical, dynamically feasible motion plans subject to spatiotemporal constraints. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework that translates NL instructions into Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and subsequently synthesizes trajectories via mixed-integer linear programming (MILP). Specifically, to generate executable STL formulas from free-form NL, we develop a reasoning-enhanced large language model (LLM) leveraging chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision and group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), which ensures high syntactic validity and semantic consistency. Furthermore, to resolve infeasibilities induced by stringent logical or spatial requirements, we introduce a specification repair mechanism. This module combines MILP-based diagnosis with LLM-guided semantic reasoning to selectively relax task constraints while strictly enforcing safety guarantees. Extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments demonstrate that the proposed closed-loop framework significantly improves NL-to-STL translation robustness, enabling safe, interpretable, and adaptable UAV navigation in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [397] [ContraMap: Contrastive Uncertainty Mapping for Robot Environment Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27632)
*Chi Cuong Le,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContraMap是一种对比连续映射方法，通过合成噪声样本训练显式不确定性类别，实现实时环境预测和空间不确定性估计，无需贝叶斯推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的机器人感知不仅需要预测场景结构，还需要识别哪些预测由于稀疏或缺失观测而不可靠。现有方法通常缺乏对未观测区域的显式不确定性估计。

Method: 提出ContraMap方法，将基于核的判别式地图与使用合成噪声样本训练的显式不确定性类别相结合。将未观测区域视为对比类别，通过简单的混合模型视图，将不确定性类别的概率视为距离感知不确定性代理的单调函数。

Result: 在2D占据地图、3D语义地图和桌面场景重建实验中，ContraMap保持了地图质量，产生了空间一致的不确定性估计，并且比贝叶斯核地图基线方法效率显著更高。

Conclusion: ContraMap能够同时进行环境预测和空间不确定性估计，提供可靠且高效的机器人感知解决方案，特别适用于需要实时处理稀疏观测的场景。

Abstract: Reliable robot perception requires not only predicting scene structure, but also identifying where predictions should be treated as unreliable due to sparse or missing observations. We present ContraMap, a contrastive continuous mapping method that augments kernel-based discriminative maps with an explicit uncertainty class trained using synthetic noise samples. This formulation treats unobserved regions as a contrastive class, enabling joint environment prediction and spatial uncertainty estimation in real time without Bayesian inference. Under a simple mixture-model view, we show that the probability assigned to the uncertainty class is a monotonic function of a distance-aware uncertainty surrogate. Experiments in 2D occupancy mapping, 3D semantic mapping, and tabletop scene reconstruction show that ContraMap preserves mapping quality, produces spatially coherent uncertainty estimates, and is substantially more efficient than Bayesian kernelmap baselines.

</details>


### [398] [ProgressVLA: Progress-Guided Diffusion Policy for Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27670)
*Hongyu Yan,Qiwei Li,Jiaolong Yang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型提出了一种结合任务进度估计和可微分进度指导的新方法，用于解决机器人操作中缺乏进度感知的问题，在长时程任务中显著提升了成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型缺乏进度感知能力，通常依赖手工设计的启发式规则进行任务终止判断，这在涉及级联子目标的长时程任务中尤为严重。

Method: 提出VLA模型，包含两个技术贡献：1）鲁棒进度估计：在大规模无监督视频-文本机器人数据集上预训练进度估计器；2）可微分进度指导：引入逆动力学世界模型，将预测的动作token映射到未来潜在视觉状态，通过进度估计器处理并应用最大进度正则化，建立可微分管道来精炼动作token。

Result: 在CALVIN和LIBERO基准测试以及真实世界机器人部署中，相比强基线模型，VLA模型在成功率和泛化能力方面都取得了显著提升。进度估计器在模拟中实现了低预测残差（0.07），并对未见过的真实世界样本展示了零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过集成任务进度估计和可微分进度指导，VLA模型有效解决了机器人操作中缺乏进度感知的问题，在长时程任务中表现出优越的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Most existing vision-language-action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation lack progress awareness, typically relying on hand-crafted heuristics for task termination. This limitation is particularly severe in long-horizon tasks involving cascaded sub-goals. In this work, we investigate the estimation and integration of task progress, proposing a novel model named {\textbf \vla}. Our technical contributions are twofold: (1) \emph{robust progress estimation}: We pre-train a progress estimator on large-scale, unsupervised video-text robotic datasets. This estimator achieves a low prediction residual (0.07 on a scale of $[0, 1]$) in simulation and demonstrates zero-shot generalization to unseen real-world samples, and (2) \emph{differentiable progress guidance}: We introduce an inverse dynamics world model that maps predicted action tokens into future latent visual states. These latents are then processed by the progress estimator; by applying a maximal progress regularization, we establish a differentiable pipeline that provides progress-piloted guidance to refine action tokens. Extensive experiments on the CALVIN and LIBERO benchmarks, alongside real-world robot deployment, consistently demonstrate substantial improvements in success rates and generalization over strong baselines.

</details>


### [399] [TerraSkipper: A Centimeter-Scale Robot for Multi-Terrain Skipping and Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27725)
*Shashwat Singh,Sheri Zhang,Spencer Matonis,Zeynep Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受弹涂鱼启发，开发了一种厘米级多地形跳跃爬行机器人，结合跳跃和爬行实现多种地形运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹涂鱼能够在陆地、水域和泥泞等粘性基质中运动，其强大的尾部能够储存并快速释放能量产生跳跃。受此生物机制启发，需要开发能够在多种地形中运动的机器人

Method: 设计并制造了3D打印的厘米级机器人，配备侧鳍用于爬行（带霍尔传感器控制步态），以及由10mm行星齿轮电机驱动的旋转弹簧尾实现连续跳跃。对尾部进行建模和实验表征，确定25mm为最佳长度

Result: 尾部最佳长度25mm产生平均推进力4N（峰值达6N）。跳跃在粘性和颗粒介质上的平均速度始终高于爬行。户外测试表明，在草地、松沙和硬地上结合跳跃和爬行可扩展机器人在真实环境中的操作范围

Conclusion: 结合跳跃（用于缠绕和颗粒地形）和爬行（用于坚实地面）的多模式运动策略，显著提高了机器人在复杂真实环境中的运动能力和操作范围

Abstract: Mudskippers are unique amphibious fish capable of locomotion in diverse environments, including terrestrial surfaces, aquatic habitats, and highly viscous substrates such as mud. This versatile locomotion is largely enabled by their powerful tail, which stores and rapidly releases energy to produce impulsive jumps. Inspired by this biological mechanism, we present the design and development of a multi-terrain centimeter-scale skipping and crawling robot. The robot is predominantly 3D printed and features onboard sensing, computation, and power. It is equipped with two side fins for crawling, each integrated with a hall effect sensor for gait control, while a rotary springtail driven by a 10mm planetary gear motor enables continuous impulsive skipping across a range of substrates to achieve multi-terrain locomotion. We modeled and experimentally characterized the tail, identifying an optimal length of 25mm that maximizes the mean propulsive force (4N, peaks up to 6N) for forward motion. In addition, we evaluated skipping on substrates where fin based crawling alone fails, and varied the moisture content of uniform sand and bentonite clay powder to compare skipping with crawling. Skipping consistently produced higher mean velocities than crawling, particularly on viscous and granular media. Finally, outdoor tests on grass, loose sand, and hard ground confirmed that combining skipping on entangling and granular terrain with crawling on firm ground extends the operational range of the robot in real-world environments.

</details>


### [400] [Heracles: Bridging Precise Tracking and Generative Synthesis for General Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27756)
*Zelin Tao,Zeran Su,Peiran Liu,Jingkai Sun,Wenqiang Que,Jiahao Ma,Jialin Yu,Jiahang Cao,Pihai Sun,Hao Liang,Gang Han,Wen Zhao,Zhiyuan Xu,Yijie Guo,Jian Tang,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Heracles是一种基于扩散模型的中间件，在精确运动跟踪和生成式合成之间架起桥梁，通过状态条件化实现从跟踪到自然恢复轨迹的无缝转换，显著提升人形机器人控制的鲁棒性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用控制器主要将运动控制视为刚性参考跟踪问题，在标称条件下有效，但在严重干扰下表现出脆弱、非拟人化的失效模式，缺乏人类运动控制固有的生成适应性。需要一种能平衡精确运动执行和拟人适应性的方法。

Method: 提出Heracles，一种状态条件化的扩散中间件，作为高层参考运动和低层物理跟踪器之间的中介层。通过基于机器人实时状态进行条件化，扩散模型隐式适应其行为：当状态与参考对齐时近似恒等映射保持跟踪保真度；当遇到显著状态偏差时无缝转换为生成合成器，产生自然的拟人恢复轨迹。

Result: 该框架表明，将生成先验集成到控制循环中，不仅显著增强了对极端扰动的鲁棒性，还将人形控制从刚性跟踪范式提升为开放式的生成通用架构。

Conclusion: Heracles通过扩散中间件实现了精确运动跟踪和生成适应性之间的平衡，为人形机器人控制提供了更鲁棒、更拟人化的解决方案，推动了从刚性跟踪到生成式通用架构的转变。

Abstract: Achieving general-purpose humanoid control requires a delicate balance between the precise execution of commanded motions and the flexible, anthropomorphic adaptability needed to recover from unpredictable environmental perturbations. Current general controllers predominantly formulate motion control as a rigid reference-tracking problem. While effective in nominal conditions, these trackers often exhibit brittle, non-anthropomorphic failure modes under severe disturbances, lacking the generative adaptability inherent to human motor control. To overcome this limitation, we propose Heracles, a novel state-conditioned diffusion middleware that bridges precise motion tracking and generative synthesis. Rather than relying on rigid tracking paradigms or complex explicit mode-switching, Heracles operates as an intermediary layer between high-level reference motions and low-level physics trackers. By conditioning on the robot's real-time state, the diffusion model implicitly adapts its behavior: it approximates an identity map when the state closely aligns with the reference, preserving zero-shot tracking fidelity. Conversely, when encountering significant state deviations, it seamlessly transitions into a generative synthesizer to produce natural, anthropomorphic recovery trajectories. Our framework demonstrates that integrating generative priors into the control loop not only significantly enhances robustness against extreme perturbations but also elevates humanoid control from a rigid tracking paradigm to an open-ended, generative general-purpose architecture.

</details>


### [401] [Transferability Through Cooperative Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27770)
*Rodrigo Serra,Carlos Azevedo,André Silva,Kevin Alcedo,Quentin Rouxel,Peter So,Alejandro Suarez,Alin Albu-Schäeffer,Pedro U. Lima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个促进机器人模块（软件、硬件、数据）在异构系统间可移植和可组合的协作竞争框架，通过任务设计、共享基础设施和基于版税的评分系统激励团队合作，并以首届euROBIN竞赛为案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人模块在不同系统间的重用和组合面临挑战，缺乏有效的激励机制促进跨团队协作和模块共享，需要建立能够平衡竞争与合作的结构化框架。

Method: 设计了包含结构化任务设计、共享基础设施和基于版税评分系统的协作竞争框架；通过首届euROBIN竞赛作为案例研究，涉及15个机器人平台在工业、服务和户外三个领域的竞争。

Result: 框架成功实施，但揭示了实际模块重用的挑战，包括集成复杂性和系统兼容性问题；通过参与者表现、集成行为和团队反馈评估了框架有效性。

Conclusion: 总结了经验教训，提出了未来协作竞争的改进建议，包括如何更好地促进模块重用和跨系统协作。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework for cooperative robotics competitions (coopetitions) that promote the transferability and composability of robotics modules, including software, hardware, and data, across heterogeneous robotic systems. The framework is designed to incentivize collaboration between teams through structured task design, shared infrastructure, and a royalty-based scoring system. As a case study, the paper details the implementation and outcomes of the first euROBIN Coopetition, held under the European Robotics and AI Network (euROBIN), which featured fifteen robotic platforms competing across Industrial, Service, and Outdoor domains. The study highlights the practical challenges of achieving module reuse in real-world scenarios, particularly in terms of integration complexity and system compatibility. It also examines participant performance, integration behavior, and team feedback to assess the effectiveness of the framework. The paper concludes with lessons learned and recommendations for future coopetitions, including improveme

</details>


### [402] [Spectral Decomposition of Inverse Dynamics for Fast Exploration in Model-Based Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27796)
*Solvin Sigurdson,Benjamin Riviere,Joel Burdick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于逆动力学方程谱分解的搜索树方法，用于规划长时程机器人操作序列，能高效处理复杂接触动力学和多接触模式问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划长时程机器人操作序列具有挑战性，因为需要探索非线性接触动力学和多种接触模式下的可行轨迹，且问题复杂度随时程增长而增加。

Method: 使用逆动力学方程的谱分解生成轨迹，该方程将执行器位移映射到物体位移，其谱分量正交且能近似物体的可达集，同时保持动力学可行性。这些轨迹可与RRT等搜索方法结合进行长时程规划。

Result: 在短时程任务上与现有方法表现相当，但在长时程任务上表现突出：现有方法失败时，本方法能在15秒计算时间内生成45秒时长、10+接触模式的计划，在高度复杂领域展示实时能力。

Conclusion: 基于逆动力学方程谱分解的搜索树方法能有效解决长时程机器人操作规划问题，在复杂接触动力学场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Planning long duration robotic manipulation sequences is challenging because of the complexity of exploring feasible trajectories through nonlinear contact dynamics and many contact modes. Moreover, this complexity grows with the problem's horizon length. We propose a search tree method that generates trajectories using the spectral decomposition of the inverse dynamics equation. This equation maps actuator displacement to object displacement, and its spectrum is efficient for exploration because its components are orthogonal and they approximate the reachable set of the object while remaining dynamically feasible. These trajectories can be combined with any search based method, such as Rapidly-Exploring Random Trees (RRT), for long-horizon planning. Our method performs similarly to recent work in model-based planning for short-horizon tasks, and differentiates itself with its ability to solve long-horizon tasks: whereas existing methods fail, ours can generate 45 second duration, 10+ contact mode plans using 15 seconds of computation, demonstrating real-time capability in highly complex domains.

</details>


### [403] [Which Reconstruction Model Should a Robot Use? Routing Image-to-3D Models for Cost-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27797)
*Akash Anand,Aditya Agarwal,Leslie Pack Kaelbling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个用于机器人操作任务的3D重建路由框架，根据输入图像自动选择最合适的重建方法，在计算成本和重建质量之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务需要不同质量的3D网格重建：灵巧操作需要精细的表面细节，而避障规划可以容忍较粗糙的表示。现有多种重建方法提供不同的成本-质量权衡，但查询所有模型计算成本过高，因此需要根据输入自动选择最合适的模型。

Method: SCOUT将重建评分分解为两个组件：(1) 视角相关模型的相对性能，通过学习的概率分布捕获；(2) 图像整体难度，通过标量划分函数估计。该框架仅对视角相关模型进行学习，因此视角不变管道可以随时添加、移除或重新配置而无需重新训练。

Result: 在Google Scanned Objects、BigBIRD和YCB数据集上，使用多种网格质量指标进行评估，SCOUT在不同成本约束下均优于从LLM文献改编的路由基线。通过机器人抓取和灵巧操作实验进一步验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SCOUT提供了一个灵活高效的路由框架，能够根据输入图像自动选择最优的3D重建方法，支持任意成本约束，适用于机器人操作任务中的多维度成本约束场景。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks require 3D mesh reconstructions of varying quality: dexterous manipulation demands fine-grained surface detail, while collision-free planning tolerates coarser representations. Multiple reconstruction methods offer different cost-quality tradeoffs, from Image-to-3D models - whose output quality depends heavily on the input viewpoint - to view-invariant methods such as structured light scanning. Querying all models is computationally prohibitive, motivating per-input model selection. We propose SCOUT, a novel routing framework that decouples reconstruction scores into two components: (1) the relative performance of viewpoint-dependent models, captured by a learned probability distribution, and (2) the overall image difficulty, captured by a scalar partition function estimate. As the learned network operates only over the viewpoint-dependent models, view-invariant pipelines can be added, removed, or reconfigured without retraining. SCOUT also supports arbitrary cost constraints at inference time, accommodating the multi-dimensional cost constraints common in robotics. We evaluate on the Google Scanned Objects, BigBIRD, and YCB datasets under multiple mesh quality metrics, demonstrating consistent improvements over routing baselines adapted from the LLM literature across various cost constraints. We further validate the framework through robotic grasping and dexterous manipulation experiments. We release the code and additional results on our website.

</details>


### [404] [Probe-to-Grasp Manipulation Using Self-Sensing Pneumatic Variable-Stiffness Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27808)
*Ngoc Duy Tran,Yeman Fan,Feng Dai,Khang Nguyen,Anh Nguyen,Hoang Hiep Ly,Tung D. Ta,Shigeru Chiba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种使用被动软硬混合夹爪的探针到抓取操作框架，通过气压可变刚度关节的自感知能力估计物体相对刚度，实现基于刚度的抓取位置选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取具有不同刚度的可变形物体是机器人领域的重大挑战。估计目标物体的局部刚度对于确定最佳抓取姿态至关重要，既能稳定抓取又不会损坏物体。

Method: 采用被动软硬两指混合夹爪，配备自感知气压可变刚度关节。每个手指由两个刚性连杆通过软气动环连接，通过内部加压实现顺从交互和可控关节刚度。通过测量气动环内部压力估计接触力，提出一种通过已知夹爪闭合位移与估计法向力关联来推断物体相对刚度的探测策略。

Result: 通过弯曲角度和压力范围内的刚度表征实验验证了自感知模型，并在实际应用中展示了刚度感知的探测-抓取能力：在具有空间变化刚度的水果上选择抓取位置。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统为刚度感知的软操作提供了一种最小化、低成本的传感方法，同时保留了探测和抓取能力。

Abstract: Grasping deformable objects with varying stiffness remains a significant challenge in robotics. Estimating the local stiffness of a target object is important for determining an optimal grasp pose that enables stable pickup without damaging the object. This paper presents a probe-to-grasp manipulation framework for estimating the relative stiffness of objects using a passive soft-rigid two-finger hybrid gripper equipped with self-sensing pneumatic variable-stiffness joints. Each finger of the gripper consists of two rigid links connected by a soft pneumatic ring placed at the joint, enabling both compliant interaction and controllable joint stiffness via internal pressurization. By measuring the pressure inside the pneumatic ring, we can estimate the interaction force during contact. Building on this, we propose a practical probing strategy to infer relative object stiffness by correlating the estimated normal force with known gripper closing displacement. We validate the self-sensing model through stiffness characterization experiments across bending angles and pressure ranges, and demonstrate stiffness-aware probing-and-grasping in real-life applications: selecting grasp locations on fruits with spatially varying stiffness. The proposed system offers a minimal, low-cost sensing approach for stiffness-aware soft manipulation while retaining probing and grasping capability.

</details>


### [405] [Safety Guardrails in the Sky: Realizing Control Barrier Functions on the VISTA F-16 Jet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27912)
*Andrew W. Singletary,Max H. Cohen,Tamas G. Molnar,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Guardrails：一种基于控制屏障函数的运行时保障机制，用于保证自主系统的动态安全，允许系统在操作域边缘安全运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主系统（从腿式机器人到自动驾驶车辆和飞机）的发展，需要在执行高性能动态运动时确保系统和环境的安全，避免造成伤害。

Method: 基于控制屏障函数理论，Guardrails提供了一种控制策略，将人类或AI操作员的指令与安全控制动作谨慎混合，以保证安全行为。

Result: 在F-16战斗机上进行飞行测试，Guardrails成功监督人类飞行员执行g限制、高度边界、地理围栏约束及其组合，确保安全的同时保持飞行员在安全时的控制权。

Conclusion: Guardrails是一种有效的运行时安全保障机制，能够在自主系统执行动态操作时提供安全保证，最小化对不安全飞行员输入的修改。

Abstract: The advancement of autonomous systems -- from legged robots to self-driving vehicles and aircraft -- necessitates executing increasingly high-performance and dynamic motions without ever putting the system or its environment in harm's way. In this paper, we introduce Guardrails -- a novel runtime assurance mechanism that guarantees dynamic safety for autonomous systems, allowing them to safely evolve on the edge of their operational domains. Rooted in the theory of control barrier functions, Guardrails offers a control strategy that carefully blends commands from a human or AI operator with safe control actions to guarantee safe behavior. To demonstrate its capabilities, we implemented Guardrails on an F-16 fighter jet and conducted flight tests where Guardrails supervised a human pilot to enforce g-limits, altitude bounds, geofence constraints, and combinations thereof. Throughout extensive flight testing, Guardrails successfully ensured safety, keeping the pilot in control when safe to do so and minimally modifying unsafe pilot inputs otherwise.

</details>


### [406] [Flip Stunts on Bicycle Robots using Iterative Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27944)
*Jeonghwan Kim,Shamel Fahmi,Seungeun Rho,Sehoon Ha,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过强化学习实现自行车机器人前空翻，提出迭代运动模仿方法，从不可行的参考运动开始训练出可行的敏捷行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要精确的参考运动轨迹，但实际中很难获得完美可行的参考运动。需要一种方法能够从不可行或不完美的参考运动中学习出可行的敏捷行为

Method: 提出迭代运动模仿(IMI)方法，通过迭代模仿先前策略生成的轨迹。从运动学或动力学上不可行的初始参考开始，逐步训练出可行的策略。在超移动车辆(UMV)自行车机器人上进行验证

Result: 从基于模型控制器生成的自碰撞桌面到地面翻转参考中，成功训练出地面到地面和地面到桌面前空翻策略。相比单次运动模仿，IMI获得更高成功率，并能鲁棒地迁移到现实世界

Conclusion: IMI方法能够从不可行的参考运动中学习出可行的敏捷行为，首次在自行车机器人平台上实现了无辅助的杂技翻转行为，为机器人敏捷运动控制提供了新思路

Abstract: This work demonstrates a front-flip on bicycle robots via reinforcement learning, particularly by imitating reference motions that are infeasible and imperfect. To address this, we propose Iterative Motion Imitation(IMI), a method that iteratively imitates trajectories generated by prior policy rollouts. Starting from an initial reference that is kinematically or dynamically infeasible, IMI helps train policies that lead to feasible and agile behaviors. We demonstrate our method on Ultra-Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a bicycle robot that is designed to enable agile behaviors. From a self-colliding table-to-ground flip reference generated by a model-based controller, we are able to train policies that enable ground-to-ground and ground-to-table front-flips. We show that compared to a single-shot motion imitation, IMI results in policies with higher success rates and can transfer robustly to the real world. To our knowledge, this is the first unassisted acrobatic flip behavior on such a platform.

</details>


### [407] [CARLA-Air: Fly Drones Inside a CARLA World -- A Unified Infrastructure for Air-Ground Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28032)
*Tianle Zeng,Hanxuan Chen,Yanci Wen,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARLA-Air是一个开源仿真平台，将高保真城市驾驶模拟（CARLA）与物理精确的多旋翼飞行模拟（AirSim）统一在单个Unreal Engine进程中，支持空中和地面智能体的协同仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济、具身智能和空地协同系统的发展需要能够同时模拟空中和地面智能体的仿真基础设施。现有开源平台存在领域隔离问题：驾驶模拟器缺乏空中动力学，而多旋翼模拟器缺乏真实地面场景。基于桥接的协同仿真存在同步开销且无法保证严格的时空一致性。

Method: 在单个Unreal Engine进程中统一CARLA和AirSim，保留两者的原生Python API和ROS 2接口，实现零修改代码复用。通过共享物理时钟和渲染管道，提供包含规则交通、社会感知行人和空气动力学一致UAV动态的光照真实环境。支持18种传感器模态的同步采集。

Result: CARLA-Air支持代表性的空地具身智能工作负载，包括协同、具身导航和视觉语言动作、多模态感知与数据集构建、基于强化学习的策略训练。可扩展的资产管道允许将自定义机器人平台集成到共享世界中。

Conclusion: CARLA-Air为空地协同仿真提供了统一的基础设施，解决了现有平台的领域隔离问题，继承了AirSim的空中能力并确保其在现代基础设施中持续发展。平台已开源发布，包含预构建二进制文件和完整源代码。

Abstract: The convergence of low-altitude economies, embodied intelligence, and air-ground cooperative systems creates growing demand for simulation infrastructure capable of jointly modeling aerial and ground agents within a single physically coherent environment. Existing open-source platforms remain domain-segregated: driving simulators lack aerial dynamics, while multirotor simulators lack realistic ground scenes. Bridge-based co-simulation introduces synchronization overhead and cannot guarantee strict spatial-temporal consistency.
  We present CARLA-Air, an open-source infrastructure that unifies high-fidelity urban driving and physics-accurate multirotor flight within a single Unreal Engine process. The platform preserves both CARLA and AirSim native Python APIs and ROS 2 interfaces, enabling zero-modification code reuse. Within a shared physics tick and rendering pipeline, CARLA-Air delivers photorealistic environments with rule-compliant traffic, socially-aware pedestrians, and aerodynamically consistent UAV dynamics, synchronously capturing up to 18 sensor modalities across all platforms at each tick. The platform supports representative air-ground embodied intelligence workloads spanning cooperation, embodied navigation and vision-language action, multi-modal perception and dataset construction, and reinforcement-learning-based policy training. An extensible asset pipeline allows integration of custom robot platforms into the shared world. By inheriting AirSim's aerial capabilities -- whose upstream development has been archived -- CARLA-Air ensures this widely adopted flight stack continues to evolve within a modern infrastructure.
  Released with prebuilt binaries and full source: https://github.com/louiszengCN/CarlaAir

</details>


### [408] [Control Without Control: Defining Implicit Interaction Paradigms for Autonomous Assistive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28079)
*Janavi Gupta,Kavya Puthuveetil,Dimitra Tsakona,Akhil Padmanabha,Yiannis Demiris,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"隐式控制"交互范式，通过用户自然行为线索而非直接输入来调整自主机器人行为，旨在降低感知工作量并保持用户控制感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着辅助机器人系统的发展，如何在自动化照护任务的同时保持用户的控制感变得至关重要。现有系统需要更好地与用户基本需求和动机保持一致。

Method: 通过两个先前开发的系统作为设计案例，探索隐式控制交互范式。对这两个案例进行新的主题分析，研究用户对交互的感知。

Result: 研究发现，有效的隐式控制设计能够降低感知工作量并保持用户控制感，主要通过系统的直观响应性、上下文感知能力和偏好适应能力实现。

Conclusion: 为辅助应用设计者提供了一套核心指南，指导何时以及如何应用隐式交互范式，以平衡自动化与用户控制感。

Abstract: Assistive robotic systems have shown growing potential to improve the quality of life of those with disabilities. As researchers explore the automation of various caregiving tasks, considerations for how the technology can still preserve the user's sense of control become paramount to ensuring that robotic systems are aligned with fundamental user needs and motivations. In this work, we present two previously developed systems as design cases through which to explore an interaction paradigm that we call implicit control, where the behavior of an autonomous robot is modified based on users' natural behavioral cues, instead of some direct input. Our selected design cases, unlike systems in past work, specifically probe users' perception of the interaction. We find, from a new thematic analysis of qualitative feedback on both cases, that designing for effective implicit control enables both a reduction in perceived workload and the preservation of the users' sense of control through the system's intuitiveness and responsiveness, contextual awareness, and ability to adapt to preferences. We further derive a set of core guidelines for designers in deciding when and how to apply implicit interaction paradigms for their assistive applications.

</details>


### [409] [$AutoDrive\text{-}P^3$: Unified Chain of Perception-Prediction-Planning Thought via Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28116)
*Yuqi Ye,Zijian Zhang,Junhong Lin,Shangkun Sun,Changhao Peng,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AutoDrive-P³框架，通过结构化推理无缝整合感知、预测和规划，解决现有VLM在自动驾驶中的局限性，实现更安全可解释的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于VLM的自动驾驶方法存在两大问题：1）有些VLM直接输出规划结果，跳过关键的感知和预测阶段，造成领域差距和决策能力下降；2）有些VLM虽然能生成感知、预测和规划输出，但采用碎片化决策方式，各模块独立运行，缺乏协同效应，影响真正的规划性能。

Method: 提出AutoDrive-P³框架，引入P³-CoT数据集促进连贯推理，采用P³-GRPO分层强化学习算法提供跨三个任务的渐进监督。系统逐步生成感知、预测和规划的CoT推理和答案，感知为后续预测和规划提供关键信息，感知和预测共同贡献最终规划决策。还引入双重思考模式（详细思考和快速思考）平衡推理效率与性能。

Result: 在开环（nuScenes）和闭环（NAVSIMv1/v2）基准测试中，该方法在规划任务上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: AutoDrive-P³通过结构化推理无缝整合感知、预测和规划，实现了更安全、更可解释的自动驾驶决策，在多个基准测试中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being adopted for end-to-end autonomous driving systems due to their exceptional performance in handling long-tail scenarios. However, current VLM-based approaches suffer from two major limitations: 1) Some VLMs directly output planning results without chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, bypassing crucial perception and prediction stages which creates a significant domain gap and compromises decision-making capability; 2) Other VLMs can generate outputs for perception, prediction, and planning tasks but employ a fragmented decision-making approach where these modules operate separately, leading to a significant lack of synergy that undermines true planning performance. To address these limitations, we propose ${AutoDrive\text{-}P^3}$, a novel framework that seamlessly integrates $\textbf{P}$erception, $\textbf{P}$rediction, and $\textbf{P}$lanning through structured reasoning. We introduce the ${P^3\text{-}CoT}$ dataset to facilitate coherent reasoning and propose ${P^3\text{-}GRPO}$, a hierarchical reinforcement learning algorithm that provides progressive supervision across all three tasks. Specifically, ${AutoDrive\text{-}P^3}$ progressively generates CoT reasoning and answers for perception, prediction, and planning, where perception provides essential information for subsequent prediction and planning, while both perception and prediction collectively contribute to the final planning decisions, enabling safer and more interpretable autonomous driving. Additionally, to balance inference efficiency with performance, we introduce dual thinking modes: detailed thinking and fast thinking. Extensive experiments on both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop (NAVSIMv1/v2) benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in planning tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/haha-yuki-haha/AutoDrive-P3.

</details>


### [410] [A Position Statement on Endovascular Models and Effectiveness Metrics for Mechanical Thrombectomy Navigation, on behalf of the Stakeholder Taskforce for AI-assisted Robotic Thrombectomy (START)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28129)
*Harry Robertshaw,Anna Barnes,Phil Blakelock,Raphael Blanc,Robert Crossley,Rebecca Fahrig,Ameer E. Hassan,Benjamin Jackson,Lennart Karstensen,Neelam Kaur,Markus Kowarschik,Jeremy Lynch,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Dwight Meglan,Vitor Mendes Pereira,Mouloud Ourak,Matteo Pantano,S. M. Hadi Sadati,Alice Taylor-Gee,Tom Vercauteren,Phil White,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C. Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究建立了AI辅助机器人用于血栓切除术的开发和验证共识框架，包括标准化有效性指标和定义跨计算模拟、体外、离体和体内环境的参考测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中风是21世纪中叶的主要医学挑战，大血管闭塞尤其致残，但有效治疗受地理限制。AI辅助机器人系统可改善血栓切除术的及时可及性，需要建立开发和验证的共识框架。

Method: 召集神经介入、机器人、数据科学、卫生经济学、政策、统计和患者倡导等领域的专家，通过孵化日、德尔菲过程和最终立场声明建立共识。

Result: 确定了四个基本测试环境各有不同的验证作用：计算模拟、体外、离体和体内。定义了两种宏观有效性指标类别：技术导航指标（前三个阶段）和临床结果指标（体内阶段）。强调了患者安全的重要性。

Conclusion: 建立了AI辅助机器人血栓切除术的开发和验证共识框架，为标准化测试平台和有效性指标提供了指导，强调患者安全是该技术发展的核心。

Abstract: While we are making progress in overcoming infectious diseases and cancer; one of the major medical challenges of the mid-21st century will be the rising prevalence of stroke. Large vessels occlusions are especially debilitating, yet effective treatment (needed within hours to achieve best outcomes) remains limited due to geography. One solution for improving timely access to mechanical thrombectomy in geographically diverse populations is the deployment of robotic surgical systems. Artificial intelligence (AI) assistance may enable the upskilling of operators in this emerging therapeutic delivery approach. Our aim was to establish consensus frameworks for developing and validating AI-assisted robots for thrombectomy. Objectives included standardizing effectiveness metrics and defining reference testbeds across in silico, in vitro, ex vivo, and in vivo environments. To achieve this, we convened experts in neurointervention, robotics, data science, health economics, policy, statistics, and patient advocacy. Consensus was built through an incubator day, a Delphi process, and a final Position Statement. We identified that the four essential testbed environments each had distinct validation roles. Realism requirements vary: simpler testbeds should include realistic vessel anatomy compatible with guidewire and catheter use, while standard testbeds should incorporate deformable vessels. More advanced testbeds should include blood flow, pulsatility, and disease features. There are two macro-classes of effectiveness metrics: one for in silico, in vitro, and ex vivo stages focusing on technical navigation, and another for in vivo stages, focused on clinical outcomes. Patient safety is central to this technology's development. One requisite patient safety task needed now is to correlate in vitro measurements to in vivo complications.

</details>


### [411] [Reducing Mental Workload through On-Demand Human Assistance for Physical Action Failures in LLM-based Multi-Robot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28156)
*Shoichi Hasegawa,Akira Taniguchi,Lotfi El Hafi,Gustavo Alfonso Garcia Ricardez,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REPAIR框架，结合LLM多机器人规划与远程人工干预，解决物理执行失败恢复问题，显著提升任务进度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多机器人协调中能将自然语言指令转化为行动规划，但物理执行失败后难以恢复，任务常因重复失败动作而停滞。现有混合现实远程操作框架很少专门针对多机器人环境中的物理失败恢复

Method: 提出REPAIR（Robot Execution with Planned And Interactive Recovery）人机协同框架，机器人自主执行任务，当发生不可恢复失败时，LLM向操作员请求协助，通过远程干预保持任务连续性

Result: 在真实环境多机器人垃圾收集任务评估中，REPAIR相比完全自主方法显著提升任务进度（时间限制内清理物品数量）。对于易收集物品，达到与完全远程控制相当的任务进度。操作员心理负荷在体力需求和努力方面可能存在差异

Conclusion: REPAIR框架成功整合LLM多机器人规划与远程错误解决，有效处理物理执行失败，提升多机器人系统任务完成能力，为人机协同机器人系统提供新方向

Abstract: Multi-robot coordination based on large language models (LLMs) has attracted growing attention, since LLMs enable the direct translation of natural language instructions into robot action plans by decomposing tasks and generating high-level plans. However, recovering from physical execution failures remains difficult, and tasks often stagnate due to the repetition of the same unsuccessful actions. While frameworks for remote robot operation using Mixed Reality were proposed, there have been few attempts to implement remote error resolution specifically for physical failures in multi-robot environments. In this study, we propose REPAIR (Robot Execution with Planned And Interactive Recovery), a human-in-the-loop framework that integrates remote error resolution into LLM-based multi-robot planning. In this method, robots execute tasks autonomously; however, when an irrecoverable failure occurs, the LLM requests assistance from an operator, enabling task continuity through remote intervention. Evaluations using a multi-robot trash collection task in a real-world environment confirmed that REPAIR significantly improves task progress (the number of items cleared within a time limit) compared to fully autonomous methods. Furthermore, for easily collectable items, it achieved task progress equivalent to full remote control. The results also suggested that the mental workload on the operator may differ in terms of physical demand and effort. The project website is https://emergentsystemlabstudent.github.io/REPAIR/.

</details>


### [412] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning Framework for Closed-loop Guidance of Fish Schools via Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28200)
*Takato Shibayama,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用深度强化学习框架通过虚拟智能体引导鱼群集体运动，在模拟中训练策略并在真实鱼群中部署，评估了视觉参数和群体大小对引导效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导生物群体的集体运动对于理解社会互动规则和开发动物管理自动化系统具有基础性挑战。本研究旨在探索深度强化学习在生物集体自动化引导中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出基于深度强化学习的闭环引导框架，使用PPO算法在模拟中训练虚拟智能体策略，然后在真实红鼻剪刀鱼中进行物理实验。设计了复合奖励函数来平衡方向引导与社会凝聚力，并系统评估了视觉参数（背景颜色、刺激大小）的影响。

Result: 白色背景和较大刺激尺寸能最大化物理实验中的引导效果。系统对5条鱼的群体展示出有效引导能力，但当群体大小增加到8条时，引导能力显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究证明了深度强化学习在生物集体自动化引导中的潜力，同时指出了在较大群体中维持人工影响的挑战，为未来研究提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Guiding collective motion in biological groups is a fundamental challenge in understanding social interaction rules and developing automated systems for animal management. In this study, we propose a deep reinforcement learning (RL) framework for the closed-loop guidance of fish schools using virtual agents. These agents are controlled by policies trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in simulation and deployed in physical experiments with rummy-nose tetras (Petitella bleheri), enabling real-time interaction between artificial agents and live individuals. To cope with the stochastic behavior of live individuals, we design a composite reward function to balance directional guidance with social cohesion. Our systematic evaluation of visual parameters shows that a white background and larger stimulus sizes maximize guidance efficacy in physical trials. Furthermore, evaluation across group sizes revealed that while the system demonstrates effective guidance for groups of five individuals, this capability markedly degrades as group size increases to eight. This study highlights the potential of deep RL for automated guidance of biological collectives and identifies challenges in maintaining artificial influence in larger groups.

</details>


### [413] [Off-Axis Compliant RCM Joint with Near-Isotropic Stiffness and Minimal Parasitic Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28240)
*Federico Mariano,Elena De Momi,Giovanni Berselli,Jovana Jovanova,Just L. Herder,Leonardo S. Mattos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于神经内窥镜操作的离轴、整体式柔性远程运动中心(RCM)关节，结合了近各向同性刚度和最小寄生运动，通过Tetra II概念设计，实现了紧凑、可制造的手术模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经内窥镜手术需要精确的远程运动中心(RCM)机构，传统机构存在视线遮挡、灭菌困难和工具更换不便等问题。需要开发一种紧凑、高刚度各向同性、低寄生运动的RCM关节。

Method: 基于Tetra II概念设计离轴整体式柔性RCM关节，采用两阶段设计：首先基于柔度各向同性目标确定移动面板尺寸，然后通过有限元可行性探索合成约束面板，在刚度各向同性和RCM漂移之间权衡。使用梁单元建模并通过详细有限元分析验证。

Result: PA12原型测试显示：刚度椭圆主轴比(PAR)为1.37，寄生与有用旋转比(PRR)为0.63%。在4.5°指令旋转下，RCM漂移保持在亚毫米级(0.015-0.172 mm)。疲劳分析预测可用旋转工作空间为12.1°-34.4°。实验验证了模拟刚度趋势，典型偏差6-30%。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种紧凑、可制造、具有近各向同性刚度和最小寄生运动的RCM关节模块，适用于受限手术通道的神经内窥镜操作，原型验证了设计的可行性和性能。

Abstract: This paper presents an off-axis, monolithic compliant Remote Center of Motion (RCM) joint for neuroendoscopic manipulation, combining near-isotropic stiffness with minimal parasitic motion. Based on the Tetra II concept, the end-effector is placed outside the tetrahedral flexure to improve line of sight, facilitate sterilization, and allow rapid tool release. Design proceeds in two stages: mobility panels are sized with a compliance-based isotropy objective, then constraining panels are synthesized through finite-element feasibility exploration to trade stiffness isotropy against RCM drift. The joint is modeled with beam elements and validated via detailed finite-element analyses, including fatigue-bounded stress constraints. A PA12 prototype is fabricated by selective laser sintering and characterized on a benchtop: a 2 N radial load is applied at the end-effector while a 6-DOF electromagnetic sensor records pose.
